    return gap_bar, 1 / (n * (1 + lam)), L, M


def adj_from_nx(G):
    G = nx.convert_node_labels_to_integers(G)
    return G.number_of_nodes(), [set(G.neighbors(v)) for v in G.nodes], G


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # ---- 实验 1 & 2：clique binary tree，按根团分解 ----
    print("== clique binary tree，按根团 R 分解 ==")
    print(f"{'(d,k,s)':>10} {'λ':>5} | {'gap(P̄)':>9} {'1/(n(1+λ))':>11} | {'损失 L':>7} | {'M':>5} | |R|·λ")
    for cfg in [(2, 4, 2), (3, 4, 3), (3, 8, 7)]:
        n, adj = clique_binary_tree(*cfg)
        R = range(cfg[1])                               # 根团 = 顶点 0..k-1
        for lam in [1, 16, 256, 1024]:
            gb, pred, L, M = decomposition_stats(n, adj, R, lam)
            print(f"{str(cfg):>10} {lam:>5} | {gb:9.6f} {pred:11.6f} | {L:7.3f} | {M:5.3f} | {cfg[1]*lam}")

    # ---- 实验 3：随机弦图上对抗搜索最大的 L ----
    print("\n== 随机弦图对抗搜索（每个图试所有极大团）==")
    rng = random.Random(7)
    best = []
    graphs = [random_connected_chordal(rng.randint(8, 13), rng) for _ in range(120)]
    graphs += [random_split_graph(rng.randint(3, 6), rng.randint(4, 8), rng) for _ in range(80)]
    for G0 in graphs:
        n, adj, G = adj_from_nx(G0)
        for lam in [4, 64, 1024]:
            for R in nx.chordal_graph_cliques(G):
                if len(R) < 2:
                    continue
                _, _, L, M = decomposition_stats(n, adj, R, lam)
                best.append((M, L, lam, n, len(R)))
    best.sort(reverse=True)
    print(f"共测试 {len(best)} 个 (图, 团, λ) 组合")
    print(f"max L = {max(b[1] for b in best):.2f}（含单团基础时间，会随 λ 线性涨）")
    print("M = t_rel(P)/(t_rel(P̄)+max t_rel(P_x)) 最大的 5 个：")
    for M, L, lam, n, r in best[:5]:
        print(f"  M = {M:6.3f}   (L = {L:7.2f})   λ = {lam:>4}   n = {n:>2}   |R| = {r}")
    for lam in [4, 64, 1024]:
        print(f"  λ = {lam:>4}: max M = {max(b[0] for b in best if b[2] == lam):.3f}")

code/chordal_diag_block_check.py

对角元与块结构的检查（配合 problems/chordal-remove-b.md §9）。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_diag_block_check.py

r"""
对角元与块结构的检查（配合 problems/chordal-remove-b.md §9）。

  命题 D′：U_y = N(y)\R 是团时，按"环境 e = J\U_y"做纤维分解，
           ⟨φ_y, Z φ_y⟩ ≤ 2 n(1+λ) Σ_e μ(e) λN(e)/Z(e)² + 2 τ_∅ Var_μ(p_e)
  引理 E ：若 U_y 与 U_y' 落在 G-R 的不相交连通分量集合里，则 Γ^Z_{yy'} = 0

用法：python3 chordal_diag_block_check.py
"""
import random
import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
from chordal_decomposition_check import (glauber_matrix, spectral_gap,
                                         random_connected_chordal, random_split_graph, adj_from_nx)
from chordal_lemma_g_check import blocker, whiskers


def blocker_k(u, k):                      # 阻挡者是 k-团 V（孪生阻挡）
    n, adj, R = blocker(u)
    v = n - 1; U = sorted(adj[v]); new = []
    for _ in range(k - 1):
        adj.append(set()); x = len(adj) - 1; new.append(x)
        for a in U: adj[a].add(x); adj[x].add(a)
        for b in [v] + new[:-1]: adj[b].add(x); adj[x].add(b)
    return len(adj), adj, R


def fiber_bound_ratio(n, adj, R, lam):
    """对每个 U_y 为团的 y，返回 ⟨φ,Zφ⟩ / [2n(1+λ)Σ_e μ(e)λN/Z² + 2τ_∅ Var(p_e)]（命题 D′ 预言 ≤ 1）。"""
    states, P, w = glauber_matrix(n, adj, lam)
    ids = [i for i, I in enumerate(states) if not any(I >> v & 1 for v in R)]
    Pe = P[np.ix_(ids, ids)].copy(); Pe[np.diag_indices_from(Pe)] += 1 - Pe.sum(1)
    mu = w[ids] / w[ids].sum(); d = np.sqrt(mu)
    S = (d[:, None] * Pe) / d[None, :]; S = (S + S.T) / 2
    Zs = np.linalg.inv(np.eye(len(ids)) - S + np.outer(d, d))       # 均值零子空间上的 Z
    tau0 = 1 / spectral_gap(Pe, mu)
    nbr = [sum(1 << u for u in adj[v]) for v in range(n)]
    idx = {i: k for k, i in enumerate(ids)}
    out = []
    for y in R:
        U = [z for z in adj[y] if z not in R]
        if not U or any(b not in adj[a] for a in U for b in U if a != b):
            continue                                                   # 只处理 U 是团
        Um = sum(1 << z for z in U)
        A = np.array([0. if states[i] & Um else 1. for i in ids]); p = A @ mu
        if not 0 < p < 1:
            continue
        lhs = ((A - p) * d) @ Zs @ ((A - p) * d)                      # ⟨φ, Zφ⟩, φ = 1_A - p
        env = {}                                                       # 环境 e = J \ U
        for i in ids:
            env.setdefault(states[i] & ~Um, []).append(i)
        pe = np.zeros(len(ids)); t1 = 0.0
        for e, lst in env.items():
            k0 = idx[next(i for i in lst if not states[i] & Um)]      # 纤维里 U 为空的那个状态
            N = sum(1 for z in U if not e & nbr[z]); Z = 1 + lam * N
            for i in lst:
                pe[idx[i]] = 1 / Z                                     # p_e = P(A | e)
            t1 += mu[k0] * lam * N / Z ** 2
        var = mu @ pe ** 2 - (mu @ pe) ** 2
        out.append(lhs / (2 * n * (1 + lam) * t1 + 2 * tau0 * var))
    return out


def max_offblock(n, adj, R, lam):
    """Γ^Z 中 U_y 落在不相交分量上的 (y,y') 的最大 |非对角元| / 最大对角元（引理 E 预言 = 0）。"""
    states, P, w = glauber_matrix(n, adj, lam)
    ids = [i for i, I in enumerate(states) if not any(I >> v & 1 for v in R)]
    Pe = P[np.ix_(ids, ids)].copy(); Pe[np.diag_indices_from(Pe)] += 1 - Pe.sum(1)
    mu = w[ids] / w[ids].sum(); d = np.sqrt(mu)
    S = (d[:, None] * Pe) / d[None, :]; S = (S + S.T) / 2
    Zs = np.linalg.inv(np.eye(len(ids)) - S + np.outer(d, d))
    nbr = [sum(1 << u for u in adj[v]) for v in range(n)]
    phis = []
    for y in R:
        a = np.array([0. if states[i] & nbr[y] else 1. for i in ids]); p = a @ mu
        phis.append((a / p - 1) * d)
    M = np.array(phis) @ Zs @ np.array(phis).T
    return np.abs(M - np.diag(np.diag(M))).max() / np.diag(M).max()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("(1) 命题 D′（纤维分解）：左/右 应 ≤ 1")
    for name, g in [("blocker u=16", blocker(16)), ("twin-blocker u=k=8", blocker_k(8, 8))]:
        for lam in [4, 64]:
            print(f"  {name:<20} λ={lam:<3}:", [round(r, 4) for r in fiber_bound_ratio(*g, lam)])
    rng = random.Random(9); worst = 0
    for k in range(120):
        G0 = (random_connected_chordal(rng.randint(7, 11), rng) if k % 2
              else random_split_graph(rng.randint(3, 6), rng.randint(3, 6), rng))
        n, adj, G = adj_from_nx(G0)
        for R in nx.chordal_graph_cliques(G):
            for lam in [4, 64]:
                for r in fiber_bound_ratio(n, adj, list(R), lam):
                    worst = max(worst, r)
    print(f"  随机弦图 / split 图：max 左/右 = {worst:.4f}")

    print("\n(2) 引理 E（块对角）：whiskers 中各 U_y 落在不相交分量上")
    for lam in [4, 64]:
        print(f"  whiskers r=4 m=2 λ={lam}: max|非对角|/max 对角 = {max_offblock(*whiskers(4, 2), lam):.2e}")

code/chordal_fiber_split_check.py

把 f 拆成"纤维平均 + 纤维内偏差"（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §13）：

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_fiber_split_check.py

r"""
把 f 拆成"纤维平均 + 纤维内偏差"（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §13）：
  A = sup E_Q(F̄)/E(f)（纤维平均放大能量的倍数），B = 纤维内偏差对中心部分的贡献。
用法：python3 chordal_fiber_split_check.py
"""
# 把任意 f 拆成 纤维平均 F̄∘pr + 纤维内偏差 g，分别测：
#   A = sup_f E_Q(F̄)/E(f)                     （坍缩到纤维平均会不会让能量变大）
#   B = sup_f 中心部分(g_c)/E(f)  [单位 n(1+λ)]  （纤维内偏差对中心部分的贡献）
# 若 A = O(1)、B = poly(λ)·O(1)，则 true ≤ 2·new·A + 2·B，修正方案成立
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import numpy as np
from chordal_decomposition_check import glauber_matrix
from chordal_mixture_means_check import build as twin_build, sup_ratio
from chordal_lemma_g_check import whiskers, blocker
from chordal_twin_blocker_combo import build as combo_build
from chordal_nested_gadget import nested_twin_gadget

def split(n, adj, R, lam):
    states, P, w = glauber_matrix(n, adj, lam); Nst = len(states)
    Lm = np.diag(w) @ (np.eye(Nst) - P); Lm = (Lm + Lm.T) / 2          # E(f) = f^T Lm f
    Rm = sum(1 << v for v in R); nbr = [sum(1 << u for u in adj[v]) for v in range(n)]
    idx = {I: i for i, I in enumerate(states)}
    ctr = [i for i, I in enumerate(states) if not I & Rm]; m = len(ctr)
    # 纤维平均算子 A_bar: f ↦ F̄ (长度 m)
    Abar = np.zeros((m, Nst))
    for k, i in enumerate(ctr):
        J = states[i]; mem = [i] + [idx[J | (1 << y)] for y in R if not J & nbr[y]]
        tot = w[mem].sum()
        for j in mem: Abar[k, j] = w[j] / tot
    Ctr = np.zeros((m, Nst)); Ctr[np.arange(m), ctr] = 1                 # f ↦ f|_center
    # 坍缩链 Dirichlet 矩阵（在 Ω_∅ 上）
    piJ = w[ctr]; ins = np.array([[0. if states[i] & nbr[y] else 1. for y in R] for i in ctr])
    C = (piJ[:, None] * P[np.ix_(ctr, ctr)]) * (1 + lam * (ins @ ins.T)); np.fill_diagonal(C, 0); C = (C + C.T) / 2
    LQ = np.diag(C.sum(1)) - C
    # 中心部分二次型（作用在 Ω_∅ 上的 F）：π_∅ Σ w_y (a_y - a_0)·F)^2
    pi0 = piJ.sum(); a0 = piJ / pi0; Qc = np.zeros((m, m))
    for k, y in enumerate(R):
        sel = ins[:, k] > 0
        if not sel.any(): continue
        ay = np.where(sel, piJ, 0) / piJ[sel].sum(); wy = lam * piJ[sel].sum()
        Qc += pi0 * wy * np.outer(ay - a0, ay - a0)
    A = sup_ratio(Abar.T @ LQ @ Abar, Lm)
    G = Ctr - Abar                                                     # f ↦ g_c
    B = sup_ratio(G.T @ Qc @ G, Lm) / (n * (1 + lam))
    T = sup_ratio(Ctr.T @ Qc @ Ctr, Lm) / (n * (1 + lam))
    return A, B, T

print(f"{'族':<26}{'λ':>4} | A=sup E_Q(F̄)/E   B=纤维偏差贡献   true")
for lam in [4, 16, 64]:
    for name, g in [("whiskers r=4 m=3", whiskers(4, 3)), ("twin s=8", twin_build(8)),
                    ("blocker u=8", blocker(8)), ("blocker u=32", blocker(32)),
                    ("twin+blocker s=16 u=8", combo_build(16, 8)), ("twin+blocker s=16 u=32", combo_build(16, 32))]:
        A, B, T = split(*g, lam)
        print(f"{name:<26}{lam:>4} | {A:12.3f}   {B:12.3f}   {T:8.3f}", flush=True)

code/chordal_glauber_gap.py

弦图上 hardcore model 的 Glauber dynamics：精确计算谱间隙（relaxation time）。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_glauber_gap.py

"""
弦图上 hardcore model 的 Glauber dynamics：精确计算谱间隙（relaxation time）。

目的：在小规模弦图上数值观察 relaxation time  t_rel = 1 / gap
如何随 (n, 分隔集大小 s, 逸度 λ) 变化，给"能否去掉 bounded separator 限制"
这个问题提供直觉。

图族：clique binary tree
    - 一棵深度为 depth 的完全二叉树，每个树节点是一个大小为 k 的团 (bag)
    - 子团和父团共享 s 个顶点（s = 最小分隔集大小）
    - 这样构造出来的图一定是弦图（它天然带一个 clique tree）
    - s = 0 时额外用一条边连接父子团，对应 Bezáková–Sun 论文里的例子（分隔集大小 1）

Glauber dynamics（heat-bath 版本）：
    每步均匀随机选顶点 v：
        - 以概率 λ/(1+λ) 尝试把 v 加入独立集（若 v 的邻居都不在集合里才成功）
        - 否则把 v 移出
    该链关于 π(I) ∝ λ^{|I|} 可逆，且转移矩阵半正定，所以 gap = 1 - λ_2。

用法：python3 chordal_glauber_gap.py
"""

import itertools
import numpy as np
import scipy.sparse as sp
import scipy.sparse.linalg as spla


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. 构造图
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def clique_binary_tree(depth, k, s):
    """返回 (n, adj)，adj[v] 是 v 的邻居集合。

    depth: 二叉树深度（根深度 0），共 2^{depth+1}-1 个团
    k:     每个团的大小
    s:     子团与父团共享的顶点数（0 <= s < k）
    """
    assert 0 <= s < k
    bags = []          # bags[i] = 第 i 个团的顶点列表
    n = 0

    def new_vertices(cnt):
        nonlocal n
        vs = list(range(n, n + cnt))
        n += cnt
        return vs

    bags.append(new_vertices(k))                 # 根团
    num_nodes = 2 ** (depth + 1) - 1
    for i in range(1, num_nodes):
        parent = bags[(i - 1) // 2]
        which_child = (i - 1) % 2                # 0 = 左孩子, 1 = 右孩子
        # 左右孩子尽量共享父团里不同的 s 个顶点（父团够大时）
        start = (which_child * s) % k
        shared = [parent[(start + j) % k] for j in range(s)]
        bags.append(shared + new_vertices(k - s))

    adj = [set() for _ in range(n)]
    for bag in bags:                             # 每个 bag 内两两相连
        for u, v in itertools.combinations(bag, 2):
            adj[u].add(v)
            adj[v].add(u)
    if s == 0:                                   # s=0: 父子团之间连一条边
        for i in range(1, num_nodes):
            u = bags[(i - 1) // 2][-1]
            v = bags[i][0]
            adj[u].add(v)
            adj[v].add(u)
    return n, adj


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. 枚举所有独立集（用 bitmask 表示）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def enumerate_independent_sets(n, adj):
    """回溯枚举所有独立集，返回 bitmask 列表。"""
    nbr_mask = [sum(1 << u for u in adj[v]) for v in range(n)]
    result = []

    def rec(v, cur, forbidden):
        if v == n:
            result.append(cur)
            return
        rec(v + 1, cur, forbidden)                       # 不选 v
        if not (forbidden >> v) & 1:                     # 选 v（若允许）
            rec(v + 1, cur | (1 << v), forbidden | nbr_mask[v])

    rec(0, 0, 0)
    return result, nbr_mask


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. 构造 Glauber 转移矩阵，计算谱间隙
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def glauber_gap(n, adj, lam):
    states, nbr_mask = enumerate_independent_sets(n, adj)
    N = len(states)
    index = {I: i for i, I in enumerate(states)}
    p_in = lam / (1 + lam)                               # 加入概率

    rows, cols, vals = [], [], []
    for i, I in enumerate(states):
        stay = 0.0
        for v in range(n):
            bit = 1 << v
            if I & bit:
                # v 在集合里：以 1/(1+λ) 移出
                j = index[I ^ bit]
                rows.append(i); cols.append(j); vals.append((1 - p_in) / n)
                stay += p_in / n
            elif I & nbr_mask[v]:
                stay += 1.0 / n                          # 有邻居被占，不能加
            else:
                j = index[I | bit]
                rows.append(i); cols.append(j); vals.append(p_in / n)
                stay += (1 - p_in) / n
        rows.append(i); cols.append(i); vals.append(stay)
    P = sp.csr_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(N, N))

    # 平稳分布 π(I) ∝ λ^{|I|}；对称化 S = D^{1/2} P D^{-1/2}
    sizes = np.array([bin(I).count("1") for I in states])
    logw = sizes * np.log(lam)
    pi = np.exp(logw - logw.max())
    pi /= pi.sum()
    d = np.sqrt(pi)
    S = sp.diags(d) @ P @ sp.diags(1 / d)
    S = (S + S.T) / 2                                    # 消除数值误差

    if N <= 2000:
        eig = np.linalg.eigvalsh(S.toarray())
        lam2 = eig[-2]
    else:
        eig = spla.eigsh(S, k=2, which="LA", return_eigenvectors=False)
        lam2 = min(eig)
    gap = 1 - lam2
    return N, gap


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. 实验
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
if __name__ == "__main__":
    lams = [1, 4, 16, 64]

    def run(title, configs):
        print(f"\n== {title} ==")
        print(f"{'depth':>5} {'k':>3} {'s':>3} {'n':>4} {'#IS':>8} | "
              + " ".join(f"λ={l}".rjust(16) for l in lams))
        for depth, k, s in configs:
            n, adj = clique_binary_tree(depth, k, s)
            out = []
            for lam in lams:
                N, gap = glauber_gap(n, adj, lam)
                t = 1 / gap
                # 括号里是 t_rel / (n·λ)：单点更新链在大 λ 下的自然尺度
                out.append(f"{t:8.1f}({t/(n*lam):5.2f})")
            print(f"{depth:>5} {k:>3} {s:>3} {n:>4} {N:>8} | " + " ".join(out))

    # 实验 A：深度 2，团大小 4，分隔集 s = 0..3
    run("A: depth=2, k=4, 变化 s", [(2, 4, s) for s in range(4)])
    # 实验 B：深度 3（状态数可控的组合）
    run("B: depth=3", [(3, 3, 2), (3, 4, 2), (3, 4, 3)])
    # 实验 C：深度 3 固定，s = k-1 随 k 增大（分隔集变大，树结构不变）
    run("C: depth=3, s=k-1, k 增大", [(3, k, k - 1) for k in range(3, 11)])

code/chordal_hardcore_dp.py

弦图上 hardcore model 的精确计算：clique tree DP。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_hardcore_dp.py

r"""
弦图上 hardcore model 的精确计算：clique tree DP。

结论：对弦图 G 和任意 λ>0，
    Z_G(λ) = Σ_{I 独立集} λ^{|I|}
可以在 O(Σ_t |C_t| · deg(t)) 时间内精确算出（C_t 是 clique tree 的节点 = 极大团），
并且可以在同样的时间内从 π(I) ∝ λ^{|I|} 中做精确采样。

关键事实：
  (1) 独立集和每个团至多相交 1 个点，所以 "I ∩ C_t" 只有 |C_t|+1 种取值。
  (2) clique tree 满足 running intersection：包含顶点 v 的 bag 构成一棵连通子树。
      ⇒ 若 x ∈ C_t \ S、y ∈ C_c \ S（S = C_t ∩ C_c，c 是 t 的孩子），
         则 x、y 不在同一个 bag 里，因此不相邻。
         所以父子之间唯一的约束来自分隔集 S。

DP 定义（自底向上）：
  V_t    = t 的子树里所有 bag 的并
  f_t(x) = Σ { λ^{|I|} : I 是 G[V_t] 的独立集, I ∩ C_t = x },  x ∈ {∅} ∪ C_t
  转移：
    f_t(x) = w(x) · Π_{孩子 c} g_c(x)
    w(∅) = 1,  w(v) = λ
    g_c(x) = f_c(x) / λ                       若 x ∈ S（x 被父子共享，权重别算两次）
           = Σ_{y ∈ {∅} ∪ (C_c \ S)} f_c(y)   若 x ∉ S（含 x = ∅）
  Z = Σ_x f_root(x)

用法：python3 chordal_hardcore_dp.py   （随机弦图上和暴力枚举对拍）
"""

import random
from fractions import Fraction
from collections import Counter
import networkx as nx


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. 随机弦图：每次新加一个点，让它连向当前图里某个团的一个子集
#    （新点的邻域是团 ⇒ 它是 simplicial 点 ⇒ 图保持弦图）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def random_chordal_graph(n, rng):
    G = nx.Graph()
    G.add_node(0)
    for v in range(1, n):
        cliques = [list(c) for c in nx.find_cliques(G)]
        C = rng.choice(cliques)
        k = rng.randint(0, len(C))                 # 允许 k=0（新连通分量）
        G.add_node(v)
        for u in rng.sample(C, k):
            G.add_edge(u, v)
    assert nx.is_chordal(G)
    return G


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. clique tree：极大团的交图上求最大权生成树（权 = 交的大小）
#    对弦图这是标准做法，得到的树满足 running intersection
#    不连通的图：权为 0 的边把各个分量的 clique tree 连成一棵树，
#    此时分隔集 S = ∅，DP 转移自动退化成乘积，仍然正确
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def clique_tree(G):
    cliques = [frozenset(c) for c in nx.chordal_graph_cliques(G)]
    H = nx.Graph()
    H.add_nodes_from(range(len(cliques)))
    for i in range(len(cliques)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(cliques)):
            H.add_edge(i, j, weight=len(cliques[i] & cliques[j]))
    T = nx.maximum_spanning_tree(H) if len(cliques) > 1 else H
    return cliques, T


def check_running_intersection(G, cliques, T):
    for v in G.nodes:
        nodes = [i for i, C in enumerate(cliques) if v in C]
        assert nx.is_connected(T.subgraph(nodes)), "running intersection 失败"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. DP：计算每个节点的表 f_t，返回 Z
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def hardcore_dp(G, lam):
    cliques, T = clique_tree(G)
    check_running_intersection(G, cliques, T)
    root = 0
    parent = {root: None}
    order = list(nx.dfs_preorder_nodes(T, root))   # 父在子之前
    for t in order:
        for c in T.neighbors(t):
            if c not in parent:
                parent[c] = t
    children = {t: [c for c in T.neighbors(t) if parent.get(c) == t] for t in order}

    f = {}                                          # f[t][x]，x=None 表示 ∅
    for t in reversed(order):                       # 子在父之前处理
        C = cliques[t]
        table = {}
        for x in [None] + sorted(C):
            val = Fraction(1) if x is None else Fraction(lam)
            for c in children[t]:
                S = C & cliques[c]
                if x is not None and x in S:
                    val *= f[c][x] / lam
                else:
                    val *= f[c][None] + sum(f[c][y] for y in cliques[c] - S)
            table[x] = val
        f[t] = table
    Z = sum(f[root].values())
    return Z, (cliques, children, f, root)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. 精确采样：自顶向下，按条件概率依次决定每个 bag 的 I ∩ C_t
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def hardcore_sample(G, lam, rng, dp=None):
    if dp is None:
        _, dp = hardcore_dp(G, lam)
    cliques, children, f, root = dp
    I = set()

    def choose(weights):                            # weights: {x: 权重}
        tot = sum(weights.values())
        r = Fraction(rng.random()) * tot
        for x, w in weights.items():
            if r < w:
                return x
            r -= w
        return x

    stack = [(root, choose(f[root]))]               # 根：按 f_root(x) 选
    while stack:
        t, x = stack.pop()
        if x is not None:
            I.add(x)
        for c in children[t]:
            S = cliques[t] & cliques[c]
            if x is not None and x in S:
                y = x                               # 共享点已被父节点决定
            else:
                opts = {None: f[c][None]}
                opts.update({y: f[c][y] for y in cliques[c] - S})
                y = choose(opts)
            stack.append((c, y))
    return frozenset(I)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. 暴力：枚举所有子集
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def brute_force_Z(G, lam):
    nodes = list(G.nodes)
    Z = Fraction(0)
    for mask in range(1 << len(nodes)):
        S = [nodes[i] for i in range(len(nodes)) if mask >> i & 1]
        if all(not G.has_edge(u, v) for i, u in enumerate(S) for v in S[i + 1:]):
            Z += Fraction(lam) ** len(S)
    return Z


if __name__ == "__main__":
    rng = random.Random(2026)

    # 对拍 Z：200 个随机弦图，n ≤ 16，λ 取若干有理数
    cnt = 0
    for trial in range(200):
        n = rng.randint(1, 16)
        G = random_chordal_graph(n, rng)
        lam = Fraction(rng.choice([1, 2, 3, 7]), rng.choice([1, 2, 5]))
        Z_dp, _ = hardcore_dp(G, lam)
        Z_bf = brute_force_Z(G, lam)
        assert Z_dp == Z_bf, (trial, n, lam, Z_dp, Z_bf)
        cnt += 1
    print(f"Z 对拍：{cnt} 个随机弦图全部精确相等（有理数运算，无浮点误差）")

    # λ=1 时 Z = 独立集个数；顺便检查一个已知值：路 P_n 的独立集数 = Fibonacci(n+2)
    P = nx.path_graph(10)
    Z, _ = hardcore_dp(P, 1)
    print(f"P_10 的独立集个数 = {Z}（应为 F_12 = 144）")

    # 采样检验：小图上的经验分布 vs 精确分布（总变差距离）
    G = random_chordal_graph(7, rng)
    lam = Fraction(2)
    Z, dp = hardcore_dp(G, lam)
    N = 200_000
    emp = Counter(hardcore_sample(G, lam, rng, dp) for _ in range(N))
    nodes = list(G.nodes)
    tv, num_states = 0.0, 0
    for mask in range(1 << len(nodes)):
        S = frozenset(nodes[i] for i in range(len(nodes)) if mask >> i & 1)
        if all(not G.has_edge(u, v) for u in S for v in S if u < v):
            num_states += 1
            p = float(lam ** len(S) / Z)
            tv += abs(emp[S] / N - p)
    print(f"采样检验：n=7, {num_states} 个独立集, N={N} 次采样, TV 距离 = {tv/2:.4f}")

code/chordal_hier_depth4.py

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_hier_depth4.py

# 深度 4（b=2）分层星套星的双精度稀疏计算（§18.5）。用法：cd code && python3 chordal_hier_depth4.py
import numpy as np, time, chordal_hier_star as H
from chordal_hier_sparse import log_trel_sparse
b=2
for dep in (3,4):
    tr=H.Tree((b,)*dep); ch=tr.chain(); g=1/(b+1); e=[b*g]+[1.0]*(dep-1)+[g]
    v=[]
    for p in (1.5,2.0,2.5,3.0):
        t0=time.time(); v.append(log_trel_sparse(tr,ch,e,np.log(10**p)))
        print(f'深度{dep} Λ=1e{p}: t·c={np.exp(v[-1]):.5g} ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s)', flush=True)
    print(f'深度{dep} 局部斜率:', ' '.join('%.3f'%((v[i+1]-v[i])/np.log(10**0.5)) for i in range(len(v)-1)), flush=True)

code/chordal_hier_mp.py

用 mpmath 高精度计算分层星套星合并链的谱间隙（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §18），

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_hier_mp.py

r"""
用 mpmath 高精度计算分层星套星合并链的谱间隙（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §18），
以便把 Λ 推到 1e12 以上，看渐近减速指数。双精度在 Λ≳1e6 时误差和间隙同阶。
用法：python3 chordal_hier_mp.py
"""
import mpmath as mp
import numpy as np
import chordal_hier_star as H
mp.mp.dps = 80


def log_trel_mp(tree, ch, e, Lam):
    """log(t_rel·c)，高精度。Lam 是 mpmath 数。"""
    N, rows, cols, coef, layer = ch
    loglam = [mp.mpf(ei) * mp.log(Lam) for ei in e]
    lw = [mp.mpf(0)] * N
    for k, t in enumerate(tree.types[0]):
        lw[k] = mp.mpf(tree.logw(0, t, [float(x) for x in loglam]))  # 对数权重的结构部分
    # 为了避免 logw 的双精度误差，直接用高精度重算：logw 是 loglam 的线性函数 + 常数（多项式系数）
    base = np.array([tree.logw(0, t, [0.0] * len(e)) for t in tree.types[0]])
    coeff = []
    for i in range(len(e)):
        unit = [0.0] * len(e); unit[i] = 1.0
        coeff.append(np.array([tree.logw(0, t, unit) for t in tree.types[0]]) - base)
    lw = [mp.mpf(base[k]) + sum(mp.mpf(round(coeff[i][k])) * loglam[i] for i in range(len(e))) for k in range(N)]
    m = max(lw); pi = [mp.e ** (x - m) for x in lw]; Z = sum(pi); pi = [p / Z for p in pi]
    lam_layer = [mp.e ** x for x in loglam]
    Q = mp.zeros(N, N)
    for r, c_, cf, ly in zip(rows, cols, coef, layer):
        Q[int(r), int(c_)] += mp.mpf(cf) * (lam_layer[int(ly)] if ly >= 0 else 1)
    for i in range(N):
        Q[i, i] = -sum(Q[i, j] for j in range(N) if j != i)
    d = [mp.sqrt(p) for p in pi]
    S = mp.matrix(N, N)
    for i in range(N):
        for j in range(N):
            S[i, j] = -(d[i] * Q[i, j] / d[j] + d[j] * Q[j, i] / d[i]) / 2
    ev = sorted(mp.eigsy(S, eigvals_only=True))
    return mp.log(1 / ev[1])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    for b, d in [(2, 1), (2, 2), (3, 1), (3, 2), (2, 3)]:
        tr = H.Tree((b,) * d); ch = tr.chain(); g = 1 / (b + 1)
        e = [b * g] + [1.0] * (d - 1) + [g]
        ps = [4, 6, 8, 10, 12] if ch[0] < 100 else [4, 6, 8, 10]
        v = [log_trel_mp(tr, ch, e, mp.mpf(10) ** p) for p in ps]
        sl = [float((v[i + 1] - v[i]) / mp.log(mp.mpf(10) ** (ps[i + 1] - ps[i]))) for i in range(len(v) - 1)]
        print(f"b={b} 深度={d} 状态数={ch[0]} 预测α={(d*b-1)/(b+1):.3f}  局部斜率（10^{ps[0]}→10^{ps[-1]}，每两个数量级）:",
              " ".join("%.3f" % s for s in sl), flush=True)

code/chordal_hier_sparse.py

分层星套星的大规模（稀疏）计算：深度 4（b=2，27652 个合并状态）。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_hier_sparse.py

r"""
分层星套星的大规模（稀疏）计算：深度 4（b=2，27652 个合并状态）。
在 d⊥ 上用 CG 求 (−S)^{-1}，Lanczos 取最大特征值 = t_rel·c。只在双精度可靠的 Λ ≤ 1e4 使用。
"""
import numpy as np, scipy.sparse as sp, scipy.sparse.linalg as spla
import chordal_hier_star as H


def log_trel_sparse(tree, ch, e, logLam):
    N, rows, cols, coef, layer = ch
    loglam = [ei * logLam for ei in e]
    lw = np.array([tree.logw(0, t, loglam) for t in tree.types[0]]); lw -= lw.max()
    pi = np.exp(lw); pi /= pi.sum()
    rate = coef * np.where(layer >= 0, np.exp(np.array(loglam + [0.0])[layer]), 1.0)
    Q = sp.csr_matrix((rate, (rows, cols)), shape=(N, N)); out = np.asarray(Q.sum(1)).ravel()
    d = np.sqrt(pi); S = sp.diags(d) @ Q @ sp.diags(1 / d); S = (S + S.T) / 2 - sp.diags(out)
    A = (-S).tocsr(); proj = lambda x: x - d * (d @ x)
    Dinv = 1 / A.diagonal(); M = spla.LinearOperator((N, N), matvec=lambda x: Dinv * x)
    def solve(b):
        x, _ = spla.cg(A, proj(b), rtol=1e-13, maxiter=500000, M=M); return proj(x)
    op = spla.LinearOperator((N, N), matvec=solve, dtype=float)
    return np.log(spla.eigsh(op, k=1, which="LA", return_eigenvectors=False, tol=1e-9)[0])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    b = 2
    for dep in (3, 4):
        tr = H.Tree((b,) * dep); ch = tr.chain(); g = 1 / (b + 1)
        e = [b * g] + [1.0] * (dep - 1) + [g]
        ps = [2.0, 2.5, 3.0, 3.5, 4.0]
        v = [log_trel_sparse(tr, ch, e, np.log(10 ** p)) for p in ps]
        sl = [(v[i + 1] - v[i]) / np.log(10 ** 0.5) for i in range(len(v) - 1)]
        print(f"b={b} 深度={dep} 状态数={ch[0]}  局部斜率 10^2→10^4: {' '.join('%.3f' % s for s in sl)}"
              f"   t·c(10^4)={np.exp(v[-1]):.4g}", flush=True)

code/chordal_hier_star.py

分层"星套星"：减速指数会不会随层数增长？（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §18）

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_hier_star.py

r"""
分层"星套星"：减速指数会不会随层数增长？（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §18）

H = 球对称树：根在第 0 层，第 ℓ 层每个点有 b_{ℓ+1} 个孩子，叶子在第 d 层。第 ℓ 层活跃度 Λ^{e_ℓ}。
由膨胀引理（§17.2），这对应一个弦图：每个点换成 m = Λ^{e_ℓ}/λ 个孪生点，时间以 c = 1/(n(1+λ)) 为单位。

合并：同一个父亲下的孩子可以互换，所以子树状态只需记录"各类孩子子树各有几个"（类型计数）。
合并链的谱是原链谱的子集 ⇒ 合并链的 t_rel 是真实 t_rel 的下界。
用法：python3 chordal_hier_star.py
"""
import numpy as np, itertools
from functools import lru_cache
from math import lgamma, log


def compositions(total, parts):
    """把 total 拆成 parts 个非负整数之和的所有方式。"""
    if parts == 1:
        yield (total,); return
    for i in range(total + 1):
        for rest in compositions(total - i, parts - 1):
            yield (i,) + rest


class Tree:
    def __init__(self, branching):
        self.b = list(branching); self.d = len(branching)
        self.types = {self.d: [(0,), (1,)]}                       # 叶子：占 / 不占
        for lv in range(self.d - 1, -1, -1):
            ch = self.types[lv + 1]; out = []
            for cnt in compositions(self.b[lv], len(ch)):
                out.append((0, cnt))
                if all(c == 0 or ch[i][0] == 0 for i, c in enumerate(cnt)):
                    out.append((1, cnt))                          # 自己占 ⇒ 孩子都不占
            self.types[lv] = out
        self.index = {lv: {t: k for k, t in enumerate(T)} for lv, T in self.types.items()}

    def logw(self, lv, t, loglam):
        """类型 t 的对数权重（含多项式系数），loglam[ℓ] = 第 ℓ 层的 log 活跃度。"""
        if lv == self.d: return t[0] * loglam[lv]
        occ, cnt = t; ch = self.types[lv + 1]
        s = occ * loglam[lv] + lgamma(self.b[lv] + 1) - sum(lgamma(c + 1) for c in cnt)
        return s + sum(c * self.logw(lv + 1, ch[i], loglam) for i, c in enumerate(cnt) if c)

    @lru_cache(maxsize=None)
    def moves(self, lv, t, parent_occ):
        """从类型 t 出发的转移：[(新类型, 速率系数, 活跃度所在层或 -1)]。速率 = 系数 × Λ_层（层=-1 表示删除，速率 1）。"""
        out = []
        if lv == self.d:
            if t[0]: out.append(((0,), 1, -1))
            elif not parent_occ: out.append(((1,), 1, lv))
            return tuple(out)
        occ, cnt = t; ch = self.types[lv + 1]
        if occ: out.append(((0, cnt), 1, -1))
        elif not parent_occ and all(c == 0 or ch[i][0] == 0 for i, c in enumerate(cnt)):
            out.append(((1, cnt), 1, lv))
        for i, c in enumerate(cnt):
            if not c: continue
            for new, coef, layer in self.moves(lv + 1, ch[i], occ):
                j = self.index[lv + 1][new]; cnt2 = list(cnt); cnt2[i] -= 1; cnt2[j] += 1
                out.append(((occ, tuple(cnt2)), coef * c, layer))
        return tuple(out)

    def chain(self):
        T = self.types[0]; N = len(T)
        rows, cols, coef, layer = [], [], [], []
        for k, t in enumerate(T):
            for new, cf, ly in self.moves(0, t, 0):
                rows.append(k); cols.append(self.index[0][new]); coef.append(cf); layer.append(ly)
        return N, np.array(rows), np.array(cols), np.array(coef, float), np.array(layer)


def log_trel(tree, ch, e, logLam):
    """log(t_rel·c)，各层活跃度 Λ^{e_ℓ}。稠密对称特征值。"""
    N, rows, cols, coef, layer = ch
    loglam = [ei * logLam for ei in e]
    lw = np.array([tree.logw(0, t, loglam) for t in tree.types[0]]); lw -= lw.max()
    pi = np.exp(lw); pi /= pi.sum()
    rate = coef * np.where(layer >= 0, np.exp(np.array(loglam + [0.0])[layer]), 1.0)
    Q = np.zeros((N, N)); np.add.at(Q, (rows, cols), rate)
    np.fill_diagonal(Q, -Q.sum(1))
    d = np.sqrt(pi); S = d[:, None] * Q / d[None, :]; S = (S + S.T) / 2
    return -np.log(np.sort(np.linalg.eigvalsh(-S))[1])


def best_exponents(tree, ch, grid=12, L1=np.log(1e2), L2=np.log(10**3.5), restarts=6, seed=0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed); D = tree.d + 1; best = (-1, None)
    f = lambda e: (log_trel(tree, ch, e, L2) - log_trel(tree, ch, e, L1)) / (L2 - L1)
    for _ in range(restarts):
        e = list(rng.integers(0, grid + 1, D)); cur = f([x / grid for x in e]); imp = True
        while imp:
            imp = False
            for i in range(D):
                for v in range(grid + 1):
                    if v == e[i]: continue
                    e2 = e.copy(); e2[i] = v; a = f([x / grid for x in e2])
                    if a > cur + 1e-9: e, cur, imp = e2, a, True
        if cur > best[0]: best = (cur, [x / grid for x in e])
    return best


if __name__ == "__main__":
    for br in [(3,), (2, 2), (3, 3), (2, 4), (2, 2, 2), (2, 2, 3), (3, 3, 2)]:
        tr = Tree(br); ch = tr.chain()
        if ch[0] > 6000:
            print(f"分支 {br}: 状态数 {ch[0]} 太大，跳过"); continue
        a, e = best_exponents(tr, ch)
        sl = [(log_trel(tr, ch, e, np.log(10.0 ** (p + 0.5))) - log_trel(tr, ch, e, np.log(10.0 ** p))) / np.log(10 ** 0.5)
              for p in (1.5, 2.0, 2.5, 3.0, 3.5)]
        print(f"分支 {br}（深度 {len(br)}，状态数 {ch[0]}）：各层指数 {e}  局部斜率 "
              f"{' '.join('%.3f' % s for s in sl)}", flush=True)

code/chordal_lemma_g_check.py

引理 G 的数值检查（配合 problems/chordal-remove-b.md §8）。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_lemma_g_check.py

r"""
引理 G 的数值检查（配合 problems/chordal-remove-b.md §8）。

比较三个量（单位都是 n(1+λ)）：
  trace  = Σ_y w_y G_y                                    原来的引理 G（迹的形式）
  opnorm = ||Γ^Z||,  Γ^Z_{yy'} = sqrt(w_y w_y') <h_y-1, Z (h_y'-1)>_{μ0}
                                                          改进的引理 G'（算子范数）
  center = sup_f [π_∅ Σ_y w_y d_y²] / E(f,f)              真正需要控制的中心部分
其中 Z = (I - P_∅)^+，h_y = 1_{A_y}/p_y。

图族：
  whiskers(r, m)：r-团，每个点挂 m 个叶子（A_y 两两独立）——迹的形式会随 r 增长
  blocker(u)    ：R = {y, r}；U = K_u 全部连到 y；一个点 v 连到 U 全部（公共阻挡点）
                   ——真实的 t_rel 会随 u 增长，饱和在 ~λ 的量级

用法：python3 chordal_lemma_g_check.py
"""
import random
import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
from chordal_decomposition_check import (glauber_matrix, spectral_gap, decomposition_stats,
                                         random_connected_chordal, random_split_graph, adj_from_nx)
from chordal_mixture_means_check import parts


def green_gram(n, adj, R, lam):
    """返回 (trace, ||Γ^Z||, center)，单位 n(1+λ)。"""
    states, P, w = glauber_matrix(n, adj, lam)
    ids = [i for i, I in enumerate(states) if not any(I >> v & 1 for v in R)]
    Pe = P[np.ix_(ids, ids)].copy()
    Pe[np.diag_indices_from(Pe)] += 1 - Pe.sum(1)           # P_∅：R 冻结为空
    mu = w[ids] / w[ids].sum()
    pi0 = w[ids].sum()
    d = np.sqrt(mu)
    S = (d[:, None] * Pe) / d[None, :]
    S = (S + S.T) / 2                                         # 对称化
    # 对称坐标下的 Z：在常数方向 d 上补成可逆后直接求解（比 pinv 数值上更稳）
    Zs = np.linalg.inv(np.eye(len(ids)) - S + np.outer(d, d))
    nbr = [sum(1 << u for u in adj[v]) for v in range(n)]
    B = []
    for y in R:
        a = np.array([0.0 if states[i] & nbr[y] else 1.0 for i in ids])   # 1_{A_y}
        p = a @ mu
        if 0 < p < 1 - 1e-12:
            B.append(np.sqrt(lam * pi0 * p) * (a / p - 1) * d)            # sqrt(w_y)(h_y-1)
    B = np.array(B)
    GZ = B @ Zs @ B.T
    s = n * (1 + lam)
    return np.trace(GZ) / s, np.linalg.norm(GZ, 2) / s, parts(n, adj, R, lam)[1]


def whiskers(r, m):
    adj = [set() for _ in range(r)]
    for i in range(r):
        for j in range(i + 1, r):
            adj[i].add(j); adj[j].add(i)
    for i in range(r):
        for _ in range(m):
            adj.append({i}); adj[i].add(len(adj) - 1)
    return len(adj), adj, list(range(r))


def blocker(u):
    adj = []

    def add():
        adj.append(set()); return len(adj) - 1

    def e(a, b):
        adj[a].add(b); adj[b].add(a)
    y, r = add(), add(); e(y, r); e(r, add())                 # r 挂一个叶子
    U = [add() for _ in range(u)]
    for i, a in enumerate(U):
        e(y, a)
        for b in U[i + 1:]:
            e(a, b)
    v = add()
    for a in U:
        e(a, v)                                               # 公共阻挡点
    return len(adj), adj, [y, r]


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("(1) whiskers：迹 vs 算子范数 vs 真实中心部分")
    for r, m in [(2, 3), (4, 3), (5, 2), (6, 1), (8, 1), (9, 1)]:
        n, adj, R = whiskers(r, m)
        for lam in [1, 16]:
            t, o, c = green_gram(n, adj, R, lam)
            print(f"  r={r} m={m} λ={lam:<3}: trace={t:6.3f}  ||Γ^Z||={o:6.3f}  center={c:6.3f}")

    print("\n(2) 公共阻挡点：真实 t_rel/(n(1+λ))、(⋆) 常数 M（R={y,r}）、||Γ^Z||/λ̄")
    for lam in [4, 16, 64]:
        for u in [1, 4, 16, 64]:
            n, adj, R = blocker(u)
            _, P, w = glauber_matrix(n, adj, lam)
            trel = 1 / spectral_gap(P, w) / (n * (1 + lam))
            M = decomposition_stats(n, adj, R, lam)[3]
            o = green_gram(n, adj, R, lam)[1]
            print(f"  λ={lam:<3} u={u:<3}: t_rel={trel:7.3f}  M={M:6.3f}  ||Γ^Z||/λ̄={o/max(1, lam):.3f}")

    print("\n(3) 随机弦图 / split 图：max ||Γ^Z|| / λ̄")
    rng = random.Random(5)
    worst = 0
    for k in range(100):
        G0 = (random_connected_chordal(rng.randint(7, 11), rng) if k % 2
              else random_split_graph(rng.randint(3, 6), rng.randint(3, 6), rng))
        n, adj, G = adj_from_nx(G0)
        for R in nx.chordal_graph_cliques(G):
            for lam in [4, 64]:
                worst = max(worst, green_gram(n, adj, list(R), lam)[1] / max(1, lam))
    print(f"  {worst:.3f}")

code/chordal_maxcorr_check.py

两块分解 {σ_R, 其余} 的常数 1/(1-ρ)（ρ = σ_R 与其余部分的最大相关系数）对比真实 (⋆) 常数 M。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_maxcorr_check.py

r"""
两块分解 {σ_R, 其余} 的常数 1/(1-ρ)（ρ = σ_R 与其余部分的最大相关系数）对比真实 (⋆) 常数 M。
配合 problems/chordal-remove-b.md §12.5。用法：python3 chordal_maxcorr_check.py
"""
# 两块分解 {σ_R, 其余} 的常数 = 1/(1-ρ)，ρ = σ_R 与其余部分的最大相关系数（HGR）
# ρ² = 算子 T(x,x') = Σ_Y π(Y|x) π(x'|Y) 的第二大特征值；对比真实 (⋆) 常数 M
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import numpy as np
from chordal_decomposition_check import glauber_matrix, decomposition_stats
from chordal_mixture_means_check import build as twin_build
from chordal_twin_blocker_combo import build   # 孪生 + 阻挡组合
def rho(n, adj, R, lam):
    states, P, w = glauber_matrix(n, adj, lam)
    Rm = sum(1 << v for v in R)
    xs = [None] + list(R)
    sig = [next((v for v in R if I >> v & 1), None) for I in states]
    Y = {}
    for i, I in enumerate(states): Y.setdefault(I & ~Rm, []).append(i)
    m = len(xs); J = np.zeros((m, len(Y)))                     # 联合分布 π(x, Y)
    for j, (yk, ids) in enumerate(Y.items()):
        for i in ids: J[xs.index(sig[i]), j] += w[i]
    px = J.sum(1); py = J.sum(0)
    K = J / np.sqrt(np.outer(px, py))                           # 归一化后奇异值 = 典型相关
    sv = np.linalg.svd(K, compute_uv=False)
    return sv[1]
print("族                    λ |   ρ_R    1/(1-ρ)   真实 M(⋆)")
for lam in [4, 64]:
    for s in [1, 4, 8]:
        n, adj, R = twin_build(s)
        r = rho(n, adj, R, lam); M = decomposition_stats(n, adj, R, lam)[3]
        print(f"twin s={s:<14}{lam:>4} | {r:7.4f} {1/(1-r):9.2f}   {M:7.3f}")
    for s, u in [(1, 8), (8, 8), (16, 16)]:
        n, adj, R = build(s, u)
        r = rho(n, adj, R, lam); M = decomposition_stats(n, adj, R, lam)[3]
        print(f"twin+blocker s={s},u={u:<4}{lam:>4} | {r:7.4f} {1/(1-r):9.2f}   {M:7.3f}")

code/chordal_mixture_means_check.py

"团分解不等式"证明尝试的数值检查（配合 problems/chordal-remove-b.md §7）。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_mixture_means_check.py

r"""
"团分解不等式"证明尝试的数值检查（配合 problems/chordal-remove-b.md §7）。

记号（R 是一个团，Ω_∅ = {I : I∩R = ∅}，μ_0 = π(·|Ω_∅)，A_y = {J∈Ω_∅ : N(y)∩J = ∅}）：
  w_y = π(Ω_y),  m_y = E_{μ_0}[F | A_y],  m_0 = E_{μ_0}F,  d_y = m_y - m_0
  G_y = <h_y-1, (I-P_∅)^+ (h_y-1)>_{μ_0},  h_y = 1_{A_y}/p_y        （Green 函数量）
  Γ   = 事件 {A_y} 的归一化协方差矩阵（Gram 矩阵）

检查三件事：
  (1) 孪生族上 (⋆) 的常数 M 是否随孪生数 s 增长
  (2) Σ_y w_y G_y / (n(1+λ)) 和 ||Γ|| 的大小
  (3) sup_f Var_w(m)/E(f,f) 及其"中心部分 / 叶子部分"（广义特征值）

用法：python3 chordal_mixture_means_check.py
"""
import random
import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
from chordal_decomposition_check import (glauber_matrix, decomposition_stats,
                                         random_connected_chordal, random_split_graph, adj_from_nx)
from chordal_glauber_gap import clique_binary_tree


# 孪生族：R = S ∪ {u}，S 的 s 个点外部邻域完全相同（都只连到子团 D = {d1,d2}）；
#         子团 C = S ∪ D；每个 d_i 和 u 各挂一个叶子。
# 测试"孪生"情形：R 中有 s 个点的外部邻域完全相同（都只连到子团 D）
# 图：R = K_{s+1}（S = R 的前 s 个点 + 1 个点 u）；子团 C = S ∪ D，D = {d1,d2}；
#     每个 d_i 挂一个叶子；u 挂一个叶子。

def build(s, d=2):
    n = 0; adj = []
    def add():
        nonlocal n; adj.append(set()); n += 1; return n - 1
    def edge(a, b): adj[a].add(b); adj[b].add(a)
    S = [add() for _ in range(s)]; u = add(); R = S + [u]
    D = [add() for _ in range(d)]
    for i in range(len(R)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(R)): edge(R[i], R[j])
    C = S + D
    for i in range(len(C)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(C)): edge(C[i], C[j])
    for x in D + [u]: edge(x, add())          # 叶子
    return n, adj, R


def stats(n, adj, R, lam):
    states, P, w = glauber_matrix(n, adj, lam)
    R = list(R)
    ids = [i for i, I in enumerate(states) if not any(I >> v & 1 for v in R)]
    Pe = P[np.ix_(ids, ids)].copy(); Pe[np.diag_indices_from(Pe)] += 1 - Pe.sum(1)
    mu = w[ids] / w[ids].sum()
    nbr = [sum(1 << u for u in adj[v]) for v in range(n)]
    A = np.array([[0 if states[i] & nbr[y] else 1 for i in ids] for y in R], float)  # A[y][J]
    p = A @ mu
    d = np.sqrt(mu); S = (d[:, None] * Pe) / d[None, :]; S = (S + S.T) / 2
    Lp = np.linalg.pinv(np.eye(len(ids)) - S)          # 对称化后的伪逆
    G = []
    Zall = w.sum()                                    # w 已归一化 ⇒ 1
    pi0 = w[ids].sum()
    for y in range(len(R)):
        if p[y] <= 0 or p[y] >= 1 - 1e-12: continue
        phi = (A[y] / p[y] - 1) * d
        wy = lam * pi0 * p[y]                          # w_y = π(Ω_y)
        G.append(wy * (phi @ Lp @ phi))                # 记录 w_y·G_y
    H = np.array([(A[y] - p[y]) / np.sqrt(p[y]) for y in range(len(R)) if 0 < p[y] < 1])
    Gam = (H * mu) @ H.T if len(H) else np.zeros((1, 1))
    return (sum(G) if G else 0) / (n * (1 + lam)), np.linalg.norm(Gam, 2), len(R)


def sup_ratio(Qm, Lm):
    # max f^T Q f / f^T L f, 在 L 的值域上（L 半正定，核为常数）
    ev, U = np.linalg.eigh(Lm); keep = ev > 1e-12 * ev.max()
    B = U[:, keep] / np.sqrt(ev[keep])
    return np.linalg.eigvalsh(B.T @ Qm @ B).max()

def parts(n, adj, R, lam):
    states, P, w = glauber_matrix(n, adj, lam)
    N = len(states); R = list(R)
    Lm = np.diag(w) @ (np.eye(N) - P); Lm = (Lm + Lm.T) / 2      # Dirichlet 形式矩阵
    nbr = [sum(1 << u for u in adj[v]) for v in range(n)]
    ctr = [i for i, I in enumerate(states) if not any(I >> v & 1 for v in R)]
    pi0 = w[ctr].sum()
    # 每个分量的均值泛函 m_x = <a_x, f>
    a0 = np.zeros(N); a0[ctr] = w[ctr] / pi0
    leaves = []
    for y in R:
        a = np.zeros(N); sel = [i for i in ctr if not states[i] & nbr[y]]
        if not sel: continue
        a[sel] = w[sel] / w[sel].sum()
        leaves.append((lam * w[sel].sum(), a))           # (w_y, 均值泛函)
    W = sum(wy for wy, _ in leaves)
    Qc = sum(wy * np.outer(a - a0, a - a0) for wy, a in leaves) * pi0          # 中心部分
    aL = sum(wy * a for wy, a in leaves) / W
    Ql = W * sum(wy * np.outer(a - aL, a - aL) for wy, a in leaves)             # 叶子部分 = W·Σ w_y (m_y - m_L)²（§14 更正：原先漏了因子 W）
    s = n * (1 + lam)
    return sup_ratio(Qc + Ql, Lm) / s, sup_ratio(Qc, Lm) / s, sup_ratio(Ql, Lm) / s

if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("(1) 孪生族上 (⋆) 的经验常数 M")
    for s in [1, 2, 4, 8, 12]:
        n, adj, R = build(s)
        Ms = [decomposition_stats(n, adj, R, lam)[3] for lam in [4, 64, 1024]]
        print(f"  s={s:>2}: " + "  ".join(f"M(λ={l})={m:.3f}" for l, m in zip([4, 64, 1024], Ms)))

    print("\n(2) Σ w_y G_y/(n(1+λ)) 与 ||Γ||")
    for s in [1, 4, 8, 12]:
        n, adj, R = build(s)
        for lam in [4, 1024]:
            g, gam, r = stats(n, adj, R, lam)
            print(f"  twin s={s:<2} λ={lam:<5}: ΣwG={g:6.3f}  ||Γ||={gam:6.3f}  |R|={r}")
    for cfg in [(2, 4, 2), (3, 8, 7)]:
        n, adj = clique_binary_tree(*cfg)
        for lam in [4, 1024]:
            g, gam, r = stats(n, adj, range(cfg[1]), lam)
            print(f"  tree {cfg} λ={lam:<5}: ΣwG={g:6.3f}  ||Γ||={gam:6.3f}  |R|={r}")

    print("\n(3) sup_f Var_w(m)/E(f,f)，单位 n(1+λ)：总量 / 中心部分 / 叶子部分")
    for s in [1, 4, 8]:
        n, adj, R = build(s)
        for lam in [4, 1024]:
            t, c, l = parts(n, adj, R, lam)
            print(f"  twin s={s:<2} λ={lam:<5}: {t:6.3f} / {c:6.3f} / {l:6.3f}")
    rng = random.Random(11)
    worst = [0, 0, 0]
    for k in range(120):
        G0 = (random_connected_chordal(rng.randint(7, 11), rng) if k % 2
              else random_split_graph(rng.randint(3, 6), rng.randint(3, 6), rng))
        n, adj, G = adj_from_nx(G0)
        for R in nx.chordal_graph_cliques(G):
            for lam in [4, 256]:
                worst = [max(a, b) for a, b in zip(worst, parts(n, adj, R, lam))]
    print("  随机弦图 / split 图上的最大值：", [round(x, 3) for x in worst])

code/chordal_nested_gadget.py

证伪实验：嵌套"公共阻挡点"结构，检验慢化是否随团大小 u 叠加。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_nested_gadget.py

r"""
证伪实验：嵌套"公共阻挡点"结构，检验慢化是否随团大小 u 叠加。

单层 gadget（见 problems/chordal-remove-b.md §8.4）：
    y —— U (u-团，全部连 y) —— v (连 U 全部)
  慢化因子约 min(u, λ)，固定 λ 时随 u 饱和。

嵌套：把阻挡点 v 本身当作下一层 gadget 的 "y"，挂 `branch` 个子 gadget：
    v —— U' (u-团) —— v'   （每个子 gadget 各一份）
  深度 d 的结构是一棵 gadget 树。它是弦图（天然有 clique tree：
  {y}∪U, U∪{v}, {v}∪U', U'∪{v'}, ...）。

判据（固定 λ）：
  若 t_rel / (n(1+λ)) 在 u → ∞ 时对每个深度 d 都饱和  ⇒ 支持猜想（每层 poly(λ)）
  若随 u 无界增长（尤其增长速度随 d 变快）           ⇒ 猜想被证伪

实现：回溯枚举独立集 + 稀疏转移矩阵 + Lanczos（eigsh）求第二大特征值。
用法：python3 chordal_nested_gadget.py
"""
import sys
import time
import numpy as np
import scipy.sparse as sp
import scipy.sparse.linalg as spla


def nested_gadget(depth, u, branch=2):
    """返回 (n, adj)。根 gadget 的 y 额外挂一个点 r（再挂一个叶子），和 §8.4 一致。"""
    adj = []

    def add():
        adj.append(set())
        return len(adj) - 1

    def e(a, b):
        adj[a].add(b)
        adj[b].add(a)

    y = add()
    r = add(); e(y, r); e(r, add())              # y 旁边的 r 和叶子

    def grow(top, level):
        """top 是本层 gadget 的 'y'，在它下面挂 gadget，并递归。"""
        if level > depth:
            return
        k = 1 if level == 1 else branch          # 根只挂一个，下面每层分叉
        for _ in range(k):
            U = [add() for _ in range(u)]
            for i, a in enumerate(U):
                e(top, a)
                for b in U[i + 1:]:
                    e(a, b)
            v = add()
            for a in U:
                e(a, v)                          # 公共阻挡点
            grow(v, level + 1)                   # v 作为下一层的 'y'
    grow(y, 1)
    return len(adj), adj


def enumerate_is(n, adj):
    nbr = [0] * n
    for v in range(n):
        for w in adj[v]:
            nbr[v] |= 1 << w
    out = []
    sys.setrecursionlimit(10000)

    def rec(v, cur, forb):
        if v == n:
            out.append(cur)
            return
        rec(v + 1, cur, forb)
        if not forb >> v & 1:
            rec(v + 1, cur | (1 << v), forb | nbr[v])
    rec(0, 0, 0)
    return out, nbr


def relaxation_time(n, adj, lam):
    states, nbr = enumerate_is(n, adj)
    N = len(states)
    idx = {I: i for i, I in enumerate(states)}
    p_in = lam / (1 + lam)
    rows, cols, vals = [], [], []
    diag = np.zeros(N)
    for i, I in enumerate(states):
        stay = 0.0
        for v in range(n):
            b = 1 << v
            if I & b:                                    # 删除 v
                rows.append(i); cols.append(idx[I ^ b]); vals.append((1 - p_in) / n)
                stay += p_in / n
            elif I & nbr[v]:                             # 被挡住
                stay += 1.0 / n
            else:                                        # 插入 v
                rows.append(i); cols.append(idx[I | b]); vals.append(p_in / n)
                stay += (1 - p_in) / n
        diag[i] = stay
    sizes = np.array([bin(I).count("1") for I in states])
    logw = sizes * np.log(lam)
    pi = np.exp(logw - logw.max()); pi /= pi.sum()
    d = np.sqrt(pi)
    rows = np.array(rows); cols = np.array(cols); vals = np.array(vals)
    # 对称化 S = D^{1/2} P D^{-1/2}（可逆链，S 对称）
    svals = vals * d[rows] / d[cols]
    S = sp.csr_matrix((svals, (rows, cols)), shape=(N, N)) + sp.diags(diag)
    # 去掉平稳方向 d 后求最大特征值 = λ_2
    op = spla.LinearOperator((N, N), matvec=lambda x: S @ x - d * (d @ x) * 1.0, dtype=float)
    ev = spla.eigsh(op, k=1, which="LA", return_eigenvectors=False, tol=1e-9, maxiter=20000)
    lam2 = ev[0]
    return N, 1 / (1 - lam2)


def run(builder, plan, lams):
    for lam in lams:
        print(f"\n===== λ = {lam} =====   （表中为 t_rel / (n(1+λ))）")
        for depth, us in plan.items():
            row = []
            for u in us:
                n, adj = builder(depth, u)
                t0 = time.time()
                N, trel = relaxation_time(n, adj, lam)
                row.append(f"u={u}:{trel / (n * (1 + lam)):.2f}")
                print(f"  depth={depth} u={u:<3} n={n:<4} #IS={N:<8} t_rel/(n(1+λ))={trel/(n*(1+lam)):8.3f}"
                      f"  ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)


def nested_twin_gadget(depth, u, branch=2):
    """变体：阻挡者是 u-团 V（孪生阻挡），子 gadget 的 U' 连到整个 V。
    这样相邻两层共享大小为 u 的分隔集 —— 正是"分隔集很大"的情形。"""
    adj = []

    def add():
        adj.append(set())
        return len(adj) - 1

    def e(a, b):
        adj[a].add(b)
        adj[b].add(a)

    def clique(vs):
        for i, a in enumerate(vs):
            for b in vs[i + 1:]:
                e(a, b)
    y = add()
    r = add(); e(y, r); e(r, add())

    def grow(top, level):
        if level > depth:
            return
        for _ in range(1 if level == 1 else branch):
            U = [add() for _ in range(u)]
            clique(U)
            for t in top:
                for a in U:
                    e(t, a)
            V = [add() for _ in range(u)]
            clique(V)
            for a in U:
                for b in V:
                    e(a, b)
            grow(V, level + 1)
    grow([y], 1)
    return len(adj), adj


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 用法：python3 chordal_nested_gadget.py          —— 单点阻挡嵌套
    #       python3 chordal_nested_gadget.py --twin   —— 孪生阻挡嵌套（相邻层共享大小 u 的分隔集）
    if "--twin" in sys.argv:
        run(nested_twin_gadget, {1: [2, 4, 8, 16, 32], 2: [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 24, 32], 3: [1, 2, 3]}, [4, 16])
    else:
        run(nested_gadget, {1: [2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64], 2: [2, 4, 8, 16, 24, 32], 3: [1, 2, 3, 4]}, [4, 16])

code/chordal_nested_gz.py

在嵌套孪生阻挡结构上计算引理 G′ 的关键量 ||Γ^Z|| / (λ̄ n(1+λ))，R = {y, r}。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_nested_gz.py

r"""
在嵌套孪生阻挡结构上计算引理 G′ 的关键量 ||Γ^Z|| / (λ̄ n(1+λ))，R = {y, r}。
用共轭梯度（CG）解 (I-S)x = b，避免稠密求逆，可以算到 ~10^5 个状态。
配合 problems/chordal-remove-b.md §10。用法：python3 chordal_nested_gz.py
"""
# 在嵌套孪生阻挡结构上计算 ||Γ^Z|| / (λ̄ n(1+λ))（R = {y, r}），用 CG 解 (I-S)x = b，避免稠密求逆
import sys, time; sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import numpy as np, scipy.sparse as sp, scipy.sparse.linalg as spla
from chordal_nested_gadget import nested_twin_gadget, enumerate_is
def gz(depth, u, lam):
    n, adj = nested_twin_gadget(depth, u); R = [0, 1]
    states, nbr = enumerate_is(n, adj)
    ids = [I for I in states if not (I & 0b11)]
    idx = {I: k for k, I in enumerate(ids)}; N = len(ids); p_in = lam / (1 + lam)
    rows, cols, vals = [], [], []; diag = np.zeros(N)
    for k, I in enumerate(ids):
        stay = 0.0
        for v in range(n):
            b = 1 << v
            if v in R: stay += 1.0 / n; continue                  # R 冻结为空
            if I & b: rows.append(k); cols.append(idx[I ^ b]); vals.append((1 - p_in) / n); stay += p_in / n
            elif I & nbr[v]: stay += 1.0 / n
            else: rows.append(k); cols.append(idx[I | b]); vals.append(p_in / n); stay += (1 - p_in) / n
        diag[k] = stay
    sz = np.array([bin(I).count("1") for I in ids]); lw = sz * np.log(lam)
    mu = np.exp(lw - lw.max()); mu /= mu.sum(); d = np.sqrt(mu)
    rows, cols, vals = map(np.array, (rows, cols, vals))
    S = sp.csr_matrix((vals * d[rows] / d[cols], (rows, cols)), shape=(N, N)) + sp.diags(diag)
    L = sp.identity(N) - S
    # π_∅ 需要完整链：π_∅ = Z_∅ / Z_total
    allw = np.array([bin(I).count("1") for I in states]) * np.log(lam)
    pi0 = np.exp(lw - lw.max()).sum() / np.exp(allw - lw.max()).sum()
    B = []
    for y in R:
        a = np.array([0. if I & nbr[y] else 1. for I in ids]); p = a @ mu
        B.append(np.sqrt(lam * pi0 * p) * (a / p - 1) * d)
    B = np.array(B)
    X = []
    for b in B:
        x, info = spla.cg(L, b, rtol=1e-10, maxiter=50000); assert info == 0
        X.append(x - d * (d @ x))
    GZ = B @ np.array(X).T; GZ = (GZ + GZ.T) / 2
    return n, N, np.linalg.norm(GZ, 2) / (max(1, lam) * n * (1 + lam))
for lam in [4, 16]:
    for u in [2, 4, 8, 16, 24]:
        t0 = time.time(); n, N, r = gz(2, u, lam)
        print(f"λ={lam} depth=2 u={u:<3} n={n:<4} |Ω_∅|={N:<7} ||Γ^Z||/(λ̄ n(1+λ)) = {r:.4f} ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)

code/chordal_split_race.py

"分裂竞争"族（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §16）：对 (⋆) 的一个反例候选。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_split_race.py

r"""
"分裂竞争"族（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §16）：对 (⋆) 的一个反例候选。

图：R = S1 ∪ S2 是 2s-团；D_i 是 u-团，与 S_i 全连（i=1,2）。clique tree 是一条路
    (S1∪D1) — (S1∪S2) — (S2∪D2)，所以这是一个区间图。
直觉：两个"相"——相 1 = {S1 有点占, D2 有点占}，相 2 对称。相 1 → 相 2 必须经过 R 全空，
     而且 S2 要能插入还需要 D2 空：
       路线 a：在 D2 空的时刻删掉 S1 的点，概率 ~1/(1+uλ)；
       路线 b：删掉 S1 的点后 D1 先抢到空位（概率 ~u/(s+u)），再等 D2 空出来。
     取 u ~ sqrt(s/λ) 时两条路线都窄，t_rel ~ sqrt(sλ)·n(1+λ)，而所有限制链的 τ = n(1+λ)。

1. 小实例：全空间精确计算 t_rel，并用 decomposition_stats 给出 (⋆) 的经验常数 M。
2. 大实例：按 S_i、D_i 内的置换对称性合并成 8 个状态（合并链的谱是原链谱的子集，
   所以合并链的 t_rel 是真实 t_rel 的下界）。τ 可以精确算出：τ = n(1+λ)（见 §16）。
用法：python3 chordal_split_race.py
"""
import numpy as np
from chordal_nested_gadget import relaxation_time
from chordal_decomposition_check import decomposition_stats


def build(s, u):
    adj = []
    def add(): adj.append(set()); return len(adj) - 1
    def e(a, b): adj[a].add(b); adj[b].add(a)
    S1 = [add() for _ in range(s)]; S2 = [add() for _ in range(s)]; R = S1 + S2
    for i, a in enumerate(R):
        for b in R[i + 1:]: e(a, b)
    for S in (S1, S2):
        D = [add() for _ in range(u)]
        for i, a in enumerate(D):
            for b in D[i + 1:]: e(a, b)
            for x in S: e(x, a)
    return len(adj), adj, R


def lumped_trel(s, u, lam):
    """合并链（8 个状态）的 relaxation time，单位 n(1+λ)。返回 (t_rel, π(相1), π_∅)。"""
    c = 1.0                                       # 速率都正比于 c = 1/(n(1+λ))，取 c=1 即得以 n(1+λ) 为单位
    # 状态 (r, b1, b2)：r ∈ {0:R 空, 1:S1 某点占, 2:S2 某点占}；b_i ∈ {0:D_i 空, 1:D_i 某点占}
    st = [(0, a, b) for a in (0, 1) for b in (0, 1)] + [(1, 0, b) for b in (0, 1)] + [(2, a, 0) for a in (0, 1)]
    ix = {x: k for k, x in enumerate(st)}
    wt = np.array([(s * lam if r else 1.0) * (u * lam) ** (b1 + b2) for r, b1, b2 in st])
    pi = wt / wt.sum()
    N = len(st); Q = np.zeros((N, N))             # Q = 生成元（连续时间速率，按合并后的类）
    def add(a, b, rate): Q[ix[a], ix[b]] += rate
    for r, b1, b2 in st:
        x = (r, b1, b2)
        if r == 0:
            if b1 == 0: add(x, (1, 0, b2), s * lam * c); add(x, (0, 1, b2), u * lam * c)
            if b2 == 0: add(x, (2, b1, 0), s * lam * c); add(x, (0, b1, 1), u * lam * c)
            if b1 == 1: add(x, (0, 0, b2), c)
            if b2 == 1: add(x, (0, b1, 0), c)
        elif r == 1:
            add(x, (0, 0, b2), c)
            add(x, (1, 0, 1 - b2), (u * lam if b2 == 0 else 1) * c)
        else:
            add(x, (0, b1, 0), c)
            add(x, (2, 1 - b1, 0), (u * lam if b1 == 0 else 1) * c)
    np.fill_diagonal(Q, -Q.sum(1))
    d = np.sqrt(pi); S = (d[:, None] * Q) / d[None, :]; S = (S + S.T) / 2   # 对称化
    ev = np.sort(np.linalg.eigvalsh(-S))
    phase1 = pi[[ix[(1, 0, 0)], ix[(1, 0, 1)]]].sum(); pi0 = pi[:4].sum()
    return 1 / ev[1], phase1, pi0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("① 小实例：全空间精确值 vs 合并链（单位 n(1+λ)），以及 (⋆) 的经验常数 M")
    for lam in [1, 4]:
        for s, u in [(2, 1), (4, 2), (6, 2), (8, 3)]:
            n, adj, R = build(s, u); sc = n * (1 + lam)
            _, tfull = relaxation_time(n, adj, lam)
            M = decomposition_stats(n, adj, R, lam)[3]
            print(f"   λ={lam} s={s} u={u} n={n:<3}: 全空间 {tfull / sc:7.3f}   合并 {lumped_trel(s, u, lam)[0]:7.3f}"
                  f"   M(⋆)={M:6.3f}", flush=True)

    print("\n② 大实例（合并链；τ = n(1+λ) 精确成立，所以 M(⋆) = t_rel/(2n(1+λ))）")
    print(f"   {'λ':>4} {'s':>8} {'u*':>6} | {'t_rel/(n(1+λ))':>14} {'M(⋆)':>8} {'√(sλ)':>8} | π(相1)  π_∅")
    for lam in [1, 4, 16]:
        for s in [10**2, 10**3, 10**4, 10**5, 10**6]:
            best = max(range(1, int(4 * np.sqrt(s)) + 2), key=lambda u: lumped_trel(s, u, lam)[0])
            t, p1, p0 = lumped_trel(s, best, lam)
            print(f"   {lam:>4} {s:>8} {best:>6} | {t:14.2f} {t / 2:8.2f} {np.sqrt(s * lam):8.1f} | {p1:.3f}  {p0:.2e}")


def nested_lumped(s, s2, u, lam):
    r"""孪生组 S（s 个点，就是 R）全连到一个分裂竞争零件的 S1'（零件参数 s2,u）。
    G−R 只有一个分量（整个零件），P_∅ 就是零件本身，τ = t_rel(零件)。
    返回 (t_rel(全图), τ) ，单位 n(1+λ)；都用合并链。"""
    c = 1.0
    st = []
    for r in (0, 1):                               # r=1：S 中某点被占（要求 S1' 空）
        for rho in (0, 1, 2):
            for b1 in (0, 1):
                for b2 in (0, 1):
                    if rho == 1 and b1: continue
                    if rho == 2 and b2: continue
                    if r == 1 and rho == 1: continue
                    st.append((r, rho, b1, b2))
    ix = {x: k for k, x in enumerate(st)}
    wt = np.array([(s * lam) ** r * (s2 * lam if rho else 1.0) * (u * lam) ** (b1 + b2) for r, rho, b1, b2 in st])
    pi = wt / wt.sum(); N = len(st); Q = np.zeros((N, N))
    def add(a, b, rate):
        if b in ix: Q[ix[a], ix[b]] += rate
    for x in st:
        r, rho, b1, b2 = x
        if r == 0 and rho != 1: add(x, (1, rho, b1, b2), s * lam * c)       # 插入 S
        if r == 1: add(x, (0, rho, b1, b2), c)                              # 删除 S
        if rho == 0:
            if b1 == 0 and r == 0: add(x, (r, 1, 0, b2), s2 * lam * c)       # 插入 S1'（要求 S 空）
            if b2 == 0: add(x, (r, 2, b1, 0), s2 * lam * c)
        else:
            add(x, (r, 0, b1, b2), c)
        if b1 == 0 and rho != 1: add(x, (r, rho, 1, b2), u * lam * c)
        if b1 == 1: add(x, (r, rho, 0, b2), c)
        if b2 == 0 and rho != 2: add(x, (r, rho, b1, 1), u * lam * c)
        if b2 == 1: add(x, (r, rho, b1, 0), c)
    np.fill_diagonal(Q, -Q.sum(1))
    d = np.sqrt(pi); S = (d[:, None] * Q) / d[None, :]; S = (S + S.T) / 2
    ev = np.sort(np.linalg.eigvalsh(-S))
    return 1 / ev[1], lumped_trel(s2, u, lam)[0]

code/chordal_trace_excursion.py

迹链的"远足分解"与孪生 + 阻挡例子的显式计算（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §15）。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_trace_excursion.py

r"""
迹链的"远足分解"与孪生 + 阻挡例子的显式计算（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §15）。

1. 引理 X（远足分解）：R 是团 ⇒ 不同叶子块 Ω_y 之间没有直接转移，于是迹链的 Dirichlet 形式精确地是
       E_tr(F) = E_∅(F) + λ Σ_y  F^T [ cΠ_y (cΠ_y + L_y)^{-1} L_y ] F ,   c = 1/(n(1+λ))，
   其中 E_∅ 是中心块内部的边（被杀死的链），Π_y = diag(π|A_y)，L_y 是 A_y 的诱导子图上的 Laplacian
   （边权与中心块相同）。check_lemma_X 在一般小图上逐项核对。
2. 孪生 + 阻挡（R = S ∪ {r}，D 是 u-团且与 S 全连，v 与 D 全连，r 挂一个叶子 q）按对称性合并后，
   中心块只有 6 个状态 (b, q)，b ∈ {e(D、v 全空), v(v 占), d(D 有点占)}，q ∈ {0,1}。
   lumped_K 用 6×6 矩阵算出迹链下的 K 和被杀死链下的 K，单位 n(1+λ)（与 n 无关，可以取到 s,u ~ 10^5）。
用法：python3 chordal_trace_excursion.py
"""
import numpy as np
from chordal_decomposition_check import glauber_matrix, random_connected_chordal, adj_from_nx
from chordal_twin_blocker_combo import build as combo_build
from chordal_trace_full_K import full_K_trace


def gen_max_eig(Qv, L):
    """sup_F F^T Qv F / F^T L F（L 半正定，只在 L 的值域上取）。"""
    ev, U = np.linalg.eigh(L); keep = ev > 1e-12 * ev.max()
    B = U[:, keep] / np.sqrt(ev[keep])
    return np.linalg.eigvalsh(B.T @ Qv @ B).max()


def check_lemma_X(n, adj, R, lam):
    """返回 max |Schur 补 − (E_∅ + λ Σ_y cΠ(cΠ+L)^{-1}L)|，应为舍入误差量级。"""
    states, P, w = glauber_matrix(n, adj, lam); N = len(states)
    Lm = np.diag(w) @ (np.eye(N) - P); Lm = (Lm + Lm.T) / 2
    Rm = sum(1 << v for v in R); nbr = [sum(1 << u for u in adj[v]) for v in range(n)]
    cen = [i for i, I in enumerate(states) if not I & Rm]; lf = [i for i in range(N) if states[i] & Rm]
    Ltr = Lm[np.ix_(cen, cen)] - Lm[np.ix_(cen, lf)] @ np.linalg.solve(Lm[np.ix_(lf, lf)], Lm[np.ix_(lf, cen)])
    # 被杀死的链：只保留中心块内部的边（对角元 = 中心内部边权之和）
    C = -Lm[np.ix_(cen, cen)].copy(); np.fill_diagonal(C, 0); E0 = np.diag(C.sum(1)) - C
    c = 1 / (n * (1 + lam)); form = E0.copy()
    for y in R:
        A = [k for k, i in enumerate(cen) if not states[i] & nbr[y]]   # A_y 在中心块里的下标
        CA = C[np.ix_(A, A)]; LA = np.diag(CA.sum(1)) - CA                # A_y 诱导子图的 Laplacian
        Pi = np.diag(w[[cen[k] for k in A]])
        form[np.ix_(A, A)] += lam * (c * Pi) @ np.linalg.solve(c * Pi + LA, LA)
    form = (form + form.T) / 2
    return np.abs(Ltr - form).max() / np.abs(Ltr).max()


def lumped_K(s, u, lam, excursions=True):
    """孪生 + 阻挡的合并计算。返回 K/(n(1+λ))（excursions=False 时是被杀死链的版本）。"""
    c = 1.0                                     # 所有边权都正比于 c，取 c=1 即得以 n(1+λ) 为单位的 K
    nu = np.array([1.0, lam, u * lam])          # b = e, v, d 的权重（d 合并了 u 个状态）
    st = [(b, q) for q in (0, 1) for b in range(3)]; ix = {x: k for k, x in enumerate(st)}
    piu = np.array([nu[b] * lam ** q for b, q in st])                    # 中心块未归一化权重
    Z = piu.sum() + s * lam * piu[[ix[(0, 0)], ix[(1, 0)], ix[(0, 1)], ix[(1, 1)]]].sum() \
        + lam * piu[[ix[(b, 0)] for b in range(3)]].sum()
    pi = piu / Z
    def lap(edges, idx):
        L = np.zeros((len(idx), len(idx))); loc = {k: j for j, k in enumerate(idx)}
        for a, b, g in edges:
            i, j = loc[a], loc[b]; L[i, i] += g; L[j, j] += g; L[i, j] -= g; L[j, i] -= g
        return L
    # 中心块内部边：权 = 较大状态的 π × c
    E = []
    for q in (0, 1):
        E.append((ix[(0, q)], ix[(1, q)], pi[ix[(1, q)]] * c))           # e – v
        E.append((ix[(0, q)], ix[(2, q)], pi[ix[(2, q)]] * c))           # e – d（u 条边合并）
    for b in range(3):
        E.append((ix[(b, 0)], ix[(b, 1)], pi[ix[(b, 1)]] * c))           # q 翻转
    allc = list(range(6)); Ltr = lap(E, allc)
    AS = [ix[(0, 0)], ix[(1, 0)], ix[(0, 1)], ix[(1, 1)]]                  # A_y, y ∈ S：D 全空
    Ar = [ix[(b, 0)] for b in range(3)]                                    # A_r：q = 0
    if excursions:
        for A, mult in [(AS, s), (Ar, 1)]:
            LA = lap([e for e in E if e[0] in A and e[1] in A], A); Pi = np.diag(pi[A])
            Ltr[np.ix_(A, A)] += mult * lam * (c * Pi) @ np.linalg.solve(c * Pi + LA, LA)
    Ltr = (Ltr + Ltr.T) / 2
    pi0 = pi.sum(); vecs, wts = [pi / pi0], [pi0]
    for A, mult in [(AS, s), (Ar, 1)]:
        a = np.zeros(6); a[A] = pi[A]; pA = a.sum()
        vecs.append(a / pA); wts.append(mult * lam * pA)
    vecs, wts = np.array(vecs), np.array(wts)
    abar = wts @ vecs
    Qv = sum(wx * np.outer(ax - abar, ax - abar) for wx, ax in zip(wts, vecs))
    return gen_max_eig(Qv, Ltr)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import networkx as nx
    print("① 引理 X（远足分解）的相对误差：")
    import random; rng = random.Random(0)
    for t in range(4):
        G = random_connected_chordal(9, rng)
        n, adj, G = adj_from_nx(G)
        R = list(max(nx.find_cliques(G), key=len))
        for lam in [0.5, 4, 16]:
            print(f"   随机弦图 #{t} |R|={len(R)} λ={lam:<4}: {check_lemma_X(n, adj, R, lam):.1e}")
    for s, u in [(2, 3), (4, 4)]:
        n, adj, R = combo_build(s, u)
        print(f"   孪生+阻挡 s={s} u={u} λ=4  : {check_lemma_X(n, adj, R, 4):.1e}")

    print("\n② 合并计算 vs 全空间计算（单位 n(1+λ)）：")
    for lam in [4, 16]:
        for s, u in [(1, 2), (4, 8), (16, 32)]:
            n, adj, R = combo_build(s, u)
            print(f"   s={s:<3} u={u:<3} λ={lam:<3}: 合并 {lumped_K(s, u, lam):.4f}   全空间 "
                  f"{full_K_trace(n, adj, R, lam) / (n * (1 + lam)):.4f}", flush=True)

    print("\n③ 大规模扫描：迹链 K  vs  被杀死链 K  vs  解析上界 6(1+λ)（单位 n(1+λ)）")
    print(f"   {'λ':>5} {'s':>6} {'u':>6} | {'迹链 K':>8} {'杀死链 K':>10} | {'K/(1+λ)':>8}")
    for lam in [1, 4, 16, 64, 256, 1024]:
        for s, u in [(1, 10**4), (10**2, 10**2), (10**2, 10**4), (10**4, 10**2), (10**4, 10**4), (10**5, 10**5)]:
            kt, kk = lumped_K(s, u, lam), lumped_K(s, u, lam, excursions=False)
            print(f"   {lam:>5} {s:>6} {u:>6} | {kt:8.3f} {kk:10.1f} | {kt / (1 + lam):8.4f}")

code/chordal_trace_full_K.py

统一刻画（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §14）：整个 K = sup_f Var_w(m)/E(f) 只依赖 F = f|_{Ω_∅}，

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_trace_full_K.py

r"""
统一刻画（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §14）：整个 K = sup_f Var_w(m)/E(f) 只依赖 F = f|_{Ω_∅}，
因此 K = sup_F Var_w(m(F)) / E_tr(F)，其中 E_tr 是 Dirichlet 形式对叶子状态的 Schur 补（迹链）。
Var_w(m) = Σ_x w_x (m_x - Σ w m)²，m_x = <a_x, F>，a_x = μ_x（x ∈ {∅} ∪ R）。
用法：python3 chordal_trace_full_K.py
"""
import numpy as np
from chordal_decomposition_check import glauber_matrix
from chordal_mixture_means_check import build as twin_build, parts
from chordal_lemma_g_check import whiskers, blocker
from chordal_twin_blocker_combo import build as combo_build


def full_K_trace(n, adj, R, lam):
    states, P, w = glauber_matrix(n, adj, lam); Nst = len(states)
    Lm = np.diag(w) @ (np.eye(Nst) - P); Lm = (Lm + Lm.T) / 2          # E(f) = f^T Lm f
    Rm = sum(1 << v for v in R); nbr = [sum(1 << u for u in adj[v]) for v in range(n)]
    c = [i for i, I in enumerate(states) if not I & Rm]; l = [i for i in range(Nst) if states[i] & Rm]
    Ltr = Lm[np.ix_(c, c)] - Lm[np.ix_(c, l)] @ np.linalg.solve(Lm[np.ix_(l, l)], Lm[np.ix_(l, c)])
    piJ = w[c]; pi0 = piJ.sum()
    A, W = [piJ / pi0], [pi0]                                           # x = ∅
    for y in R:
        a = np.array([0. if states[i] & nbr[y] else 1. for i in c])
        if a @ piJ > 0:
            A.append(a * piJ / (a @ piJ)); W.append(lam * (a @ piJ))   # w_y = π(Ω_y) = λ π(A_y)
    A, W = np.array(A), np.array(W)
    abar = W @ A                                                        # Σ_x w_x a_x
    Qv = sum(wx * np.outer(ax - abar, ax - abar) for wx, ax in zip(W, A))   # Var_w(m) = F^T Qv F
    ev, U = np.linalg.eigh(Ltr); keep = ev > 1e-12 * ev.max()
    B = U[:, keep] / np.sqrt(ev[keep])
    return np.linalg.eigvalsh(B.T @ Qv @ B).max()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("（单位 n(1+λ)）  迹链给出的 K   vs   直接在全空间上算的 K")
    for lam in [4, 16]:
        for name, g in [("whiskers r=4 m=3", whiskers(4, 3)), ("twin s=8", twin_build(8)),
                        ("blocker u=8", blocker(8)), ("blocker u=32", blocker(32)),
                        ("twin+blocker s=16 u=32", combo_build(16, 32))]:
            n, adj, R = g; sc = n * (1 + lam)
            kt = full_K_trace(n, adj, R, lam) / sc; kd = parts(n, adj, R, lam)[0]
            print(f"  {name:<24}λ={lam:<3}: {kt:8.4f}   {kd:8.4f}", flush=True)

code/chordal_trace_identity.py

恒等式检验（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §13）：

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_trace_identity.py

r"""
恒等式检验（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §13）：
  sup_f 中心部分(f)/E(f) = π_∅ ||Γ^tr||，其中 E_tr 是 Dirichlet 形式对叶子状态的 Schur 补（= Ω_∅ 上的迹链）。
用法：python3 chordal_trace_identity.py
"""
# 恒等式检验：sup_f 中心部分(f)/E(f) = ||Γ^{tr}||，
#   E_tr = Dirichlet 形式的 Schur 补（把叶子状态消去 = 迹链），Γ^{tr}_{yy'} = sqrt(w w') <φ_y, Z_tr φ_y'>
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import numpy as np
from chordal_decomposition_check import glauber_matrix
from chordal_mixture_means_check import build as twin_build, parts
from chordal_lemma_g_check import whiskers, blocker
from chordal_twin_blocker_combo import build as combo_build
def trace_gamma(n, adj, R, lam):
    states, P, w = glauber_matrix(n, adj, lam); Nst = len(states)
    Lm = np.diag(w) @ (np.eye(Nst) - P); Lm = (Lm + Lm.T) / 2
    Rm = sum(1 << v for v in R); nbr = [sum(1 << u for u in adj[v]) for v in range(n)]
    c = [i for i, I in enumerate(states) if not I & Rm]; l = [i for i in range(Nst) if states[i] & Rm]
    Ltr = Lm[np.ix_(c, c)] - Lm[np.ix_(c, l)] @ np.linalg.solve(Lm[np.ix_(l, l)], Lm[np.ix_(l, c)])   # Schur 补
    piJ = w[c]; pi0 = piJ.sum(); mu0 = piJ / pi0
    vecs = []
    for y in R:
        a = np.array([0. if states[i] & nbr[y] else 1. for i in c]); p = a @ mu0
        if 0 < p < 1: vecs.append(np.sqrt(lam * pi0 * p) * mu0 * (a / p - 1))      # √w_y · μ0 φ_y
    B = np.array(vecs)
    X = np.linalg.lstsq(Ltr, B.T, rcond=None)[0]                                     # Ltr x = μ0φ（总和为 0）
    G = B @ X; return pi0 * np.linalg.norm((G + G.T) / 2, 2)        # 中心部分前面的 π_∅ 因子
for lam in [4, 16]:
    for name, g in [("whiskers r=4 m=3", whiskers(4, 3)), ("twin s=8", twin_build(8)), ("blocker u=8", blocker(8)),
                    ("blocker u=32", blocker(32)), ("twin+blocker s=16 u=32", combo_build(16, 32))]:
        n, adj, R = g; sc = n * (1 + lam)
        tr = trace_gamma(n, adj, R, lam) / sc; true = parts(n, adj, R, lam)[1]
        print(f"{name:<24}{lam:>4} | ||Γ^tr||={tr:8.4f}   true={true:8.4f}", flush=True)

code/chordal_tree_blowup.py

树的"孪生膨胀"（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §17）：损失会不会沿 clique tree 逐层相乘？

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_tree_blowup.py

r"""
树的"孪生膨胀"（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §17）：损失会不会沿 clique tree 逐层相乘？

归约（膨胀引理）：把加权图 H 的每个点 k 换成一个 m_k 点的团（孪生类），H 的每条边换成两团之间的完全二部图。
  - H 是弦图 ⇒ 膨胀图是弦图（树 ⇒ 每条边变成一个 2m 团，clique tree 就是 H 的线图结构）。
  - 按类内置换对称性合并后，Glauber 恰好是 H 上的 hardcore 动力学：
        类 k 插入速率 Λ_k c（Λ_k = m_k λ），删除速率 c，   c = 1/(n(1+λ))。
  - 合并链的谱是原链谱的子集，所以合并链的 t_rel 是真实 t_rel 的下界；
    真实 t_rel 还至少是 1/c（类内"哪个孪生被占"的模式）。
本文件：H = 深度 h 的完全二叉树，内部点活跃度 Λ，叶子活跃度 Λ_b（Λ_b 很大 = "叶子全占"边界）。
再按"交换左右子树"的自同构合并（深度 4 时 27652 个轨道），精确求 t_rel（单位 1/c = n(1+λ)）。
用法：python3 chordal_tree_blowup.py
"""
import numpy as np, scipy.sparse as sp, scipy.sparse.linalg as spla
from functools import lru_cache


def orbits(h):
    """深度 h 子树的所有轨道（规范形）。规范形 = (占?, 左规范形, 右规范形)，左右按排序。叶子：(占?,)。"""
    if h == 0:
        return [(0,), (1,)]
    sub = orbits(h - 1); out = []
    for i, a in enumerate(sub):
        for b in sub[i:]:
            pair = tuple(sorted((a, b)))                      # 与 canon 的排序一致
            out.append((0,) + pair)
            if a[0] == 0 and b[0] == 0:
                out.append((1,) + pair)
    return out


def canon(t):
    if len(t) == 1: return t
    a, b = canon(t[1]), canon(t[2])
    return (t[0],) + ((a, b) if a <= b else (b, a))


def flips(t, parent_occ):
    """枚举在规范形 t 上翻转一个点的所有转移，产出 (新子树, 类型)。类型：0=删除，1=插入内部点，2=插入叶子。"""
    occ = t[0]
    if occ:
        yield (0,) + t[1:], 0
    else:
        kids_free = len(t) == 1 or (t[1][0] == 0 and t[2][0] == 0)
        if not parent_occ and kids_free:
            yield (1,) + t[1:], (2 if len(t) == 1 else 1)
    if len(t) > 1:
        for side in (1, 2):
            for new, kind in flips(t[side], occ):
                s = list(t); s[side] = new; yield tuple(s), kind


def counts(t):
    """(内部被占点数, 叶子被占点数)。"""
    if len(t) == 1: return (0, t[0])
    a, b = counts(t[1]), counts(t[2]); return (t[0] + a[0] + b[0], a[1] + b[1])


def size(t):
    if len(t) == 1: return 1
    s = size(t[1]) * size(t[2]); return s * (2 if t[1] != t[2] else 1)


_struct = {}
def structure(h):
    """与 Λ 无关的结构：轨道、每个轨道的 (内部数, 叶子数, log 轨道大小)，以及转移列表。只建一次。"""
    if h not in _struct:
        O = orbits(h); ix = {o: k for k, o in enumerate(O)}
        cnt = np.array([counts(o) for o in O]); lsz = np.log([size(o) for o in O])
        rows, cols, kinds = [], [], []
        for k, o in enumerate(O):
            for new, kind in flips(o, 0):
                rows.append(k); cols.append(ix[canon(new)]); kinds.append(kind)
        _struct[h] = (len(O), cnt, lsz, np.array(rows), np.array(cols), np.array(kinds))
    return _struct[h]


def trel(h, Lam, Lamb):
    """合并链的 relaxation time（单位 1/c）。"""
    N, cnt, lsz, rows, cols, kinds = structure(h)
    lw = cnt[:, 0] * np.log(Lam) + cnt[:, 1] * np.log(Lamb) + lsz; lw -= lw.max()
    pi = np.exp(lw); pi /= pi.sum()
    vals = np.choose(kinds, [1.0, Lam, Lamb])                       # 删除速率 c=1，插入速率 Λ 或 Λ_b
    Q = sp.csr_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(N, N))
    out = np.asarray(Q.sum(1)).ravel()
    d = np.sqrt(pi); S = sp.diags(d) @ Q @ sp.diags(1 / d); S = (S + S.T) / 2 - sp.diags(out)
    if N < 3000:
        return 1 / np.sort(np.linalg.eigvalsh(-S.toarray()))[1]
    # 大规模：在与平稳向量 d 正交的子空间上，用 CG 求解 (−S)x = b 作为算子 (−S)^{-1}，
    # 再用 Lanczos 求它的最大特征值 = 1/谱间隙（−S 半正定，零空间只有 d）
    A = (-S).tocsr(); proj = lambda x: x - d * (d @ x)
    Dinv = 1 / np.maximum(A.diagonal(), 1e-300)
    M = spla.LinearOperator((N, N), matvec=lambda x: Dinv * x)          # Jacobi 预条件
    def solve(b):
        x, info = spla.cg(A, proj(b), rtol=1e-12, maxiter=200000, M=M)
        return proj(x)
    op = spla.LinearOperator((N, N), matvec=solve, dtype=float)
    return spla.eigsh(op, k=1, which="LA", return_eigenvectors=False, tol=1e-8)[0]



if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("t_rel（单位 n(1+λ)；括号里是相邻深度之比）")
    for Lamb_mode in ["free", "boundary"]:
        print(f"\n叶子边界：{'自由（叶子活跃度 = Λ）' if Lamb_mode == 'free' else '叶子活跃度 1e6（叶子全占）'}")
        for Lam in [4, 16, 64, 256, 1024]:
            Lamb = Lam if Lamb_mode == "free" else 1e6
            ts = [max(1.0, trel(h, Lam, Lamb)) for h in range(1, 5)]
            ratio = " ".join(f"{t:9.2f}" + (f"(×{t / ts[i - 1]:5.2f})" if i else " " * 8) for i, t in enumerate(ts))
            print(f"  Λ={Lam:<5} h=1..4: {ratio}", flush=True)

code/chordal_twin_blocker_combo.py

"孪生 + 阻挡"组合（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §12）：

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_twin_blocker_combo.py

r"""
"孪生 + 阻挡"组合（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §12）：
  R = S（s-团）∪ {r}；D = u-团，与 S 全连；v 与 D 全连。
检查：真实 t_rel、||Γ^Z||/(λ̄(n(1+λ)+τ_∅))（引理 G′ 的量）、真实 K 及其中心/叶子部分、(⋆) 常数 M。
用法：python3 chordal_twin_blocker_combo.py
"""
# "孪生 + 阻挡"组合：R = S（s-团）∪ {r}；D = u-团，与 S 全连；v 与 D 全连（公共阻挡点）
# 检查：真实 t_rel、τ_∅、||Γ^Z||（R 的团分解），看 G′ 是否被破坏
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import numpy as np, scipy.sparse as sp, scipy.sparse.linalg as spla
from chordal_nested_gadget import enumerate_is, relaxation_time
def build(s, u):
    adj = []
    def add(): adj.append(set()); return len(adj) - 1
    def e(a, b): adj[a].add(b); adj[b].add(a)
    S = [add() for _ in range(s)]; r = add(); R = S + [r]
    for i, a in enumerate(R):
        for b in R[i + 1:]: e(a, b)
    e(r, add())                                   # r 挂叶子
    D = [add() for _ in range(u)]
    for i, a in enumerate(D):
        for b in D[i + 1:]: e(a, b)
        for x in S: e(x, a)
    v = add()
    for a in D: e(a, v)
    return len(adj), adj, R
def gz_and_tau(n, adj, R, lam):
    states, nbr = enumerate_is(n, adj)
    Rm = sum(1 << v for v in R)
    ids = [I for I in states if not I & Rm]; idx = {I: k for k, I in enumerate(ids)}
    N = len(ids); p_in = lam / (1 + lam); rows, cols, vals = [], [], []; diag = np.zeros(N)
    for k, I in enumerate(ids):
        stay = 0.0
        for v in range(n):
            b = 1 << v
            if Rm >> v & 1: stay += 1 / n; continue
            if I & b: rows.append(k); cols.append(idx[I ^ b]); vals.append((1 - p_in) / n); stay += p_in / n
            elif I & nbr[v]: stay += 1 / n
            else: rows.append(k); cols.append(idx[I | b]); vals.append(p_in / n); stay += (1 - p_in) / n
        diag[k] = stay
    lw = np.array([bin(I).count("1") for I in ids]) * np.log(lam)
    allw = np.array([bin(I).count("1") for I in states]) * np.log(lam)
    mu = np.exp(lw - lw.max()); Z0 = mu.sum(); mu /= Z0; d = np.sqrt(mu)
    pi0 = Z0 / np.exp(allw - lw.max()).sum()
    rows, cols, vals = map(np.array, (rows, cols, vals))
    S = sp.csr_matrix((vals * d[rows] / d[cols], (rows, cols)), shape=(N, N)) + sp.diags(diag)
    L = (sp.identity(N) - S).tocsr()
    ev = spla.eigsh(spla.LinearOperator((N, N), matvec=lambda x: S @ x - d * (d @ x)), k=1, which="LA", return_eigenvectors=False)[0]
    tau0 = 1 / (1 - ev)
    B, X = [], []
    for y in R:
        a = np.array([0. if I & nbr[y] else 1. for I in ids]); p = a @ mu
        b = np.sqrt(lam * pi0 * p) * (a / p - 1) * d; B.append(b)
        x, info = spla.cg(L, b, rtol=1e-11, maxiter=100000); X.append(x - d * (d @ x))
    GZ = np.array(B) @ np.array(X).T; GZ = (GZ + GZ.T) / 2
    return np.linalg.norm(GZ, 2), tau0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    from chordal_mixture_means_check import parts
    from chordal_decomposition_check import decomposition_stats
    print(f"{'s':>3} {'u':>3} {'λ':>4} | t_rel/(n(1+λ))  ||Γ^Z||/(λ̄(n(1+λ)+τ_∅)) | K/(n(1+λ)) 中心 叶子 | M(⋆)")
    for lam in [4, 16]:
        for s, u in [(1, 2), (1, 32), (4, 8), (4, 32), (16, 2), (16, 8), (16, 32)]:
            n, adj, R = build(s, u); sc = n * (1 + lam)
            _, trel = relaxation_time(n, adj, lam)
            g, t0 = gz_and_tau(n, adj, R, lam)
            t, c, l = parts(n, adj, R, lam)
            M = decomposition_stats(n, adj, R, lam)[3]
            print(f"{s:>3} {u:>3} {lam:>4} | {trel/sc:12.3f}  {g/(max(1, lam)*(sc+t0)):14.4f}"
                  f"          | {t:7.3f} {c:6.3f} {l:6.3f} | {M:6.3f}", flush=True)

code/chordal_weighted_exponent.py

加权 hardcore 的"减速指数"搜索（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §17）。

运行：cd code && python3 chordal_weighted_exponent.py

r"""
加权 hardcore 的"减速指数"搜索（problems/chordal-remove-b.md §17）。

由膨胀引理（chordal_tree_blowup.py），孪生类大小为 m_k 的弦图 ⇔ H 上活跃度 Λ_k = m_k λ 的 hardcore 动力学
（插入速率 Λ_k c、删除速率 c）。令 Λ_k = Λ^{e_k}，e_k ∈ [0,1]，看 t_rel·c 随 Λ 的增长指数
    α(H, e) = d log(t_rel c) / d log Λ    （用 Λ=10^6 与 10^9 两点的斜率估计）。
问题：max_{|H|=k, e} α 会不会随 k 无界增长？分裂竞争（4 点路径，e=(1/2,1,1,1/2)）给出 α=1/2。
H 取所有 k 点无标号树（networkx），以及若干随机弦图；e 在网格 {0,1/8,…,1} 上做随机重启 + 坐标搜索。
用法：python3 chordal_weighted_exponent.py
"""
import numpy as np, networkx as nx, itertools, random


def chain(H):
    """预计算：H 的所有独立集、转移（类型：删除 / 插入点 v）。返回可快速按活跃度重算的结构。"""
    k = H.number_of_nodes(); nb = [sum(1 << u for u in H[v]) for v in range(k)]
    st = [I for I in range(1 << k) if all(not (I >> v & 1 and I & nb[v]) for v in range(k))]
    ix = {I: j for j, I in enumerate(st)}
    occ = np.array([[I >> v & 1 for v in range(k)] for I in st], dtype=float)
    dele, ins = [], []
    for j, I in enumerate(st):
        for v in range(k):
            if I >> v & 1: dele.append((j, ix[I ^ (1 << v)]))
            elif not I & nb[v]: ins.append((j, ix[I | (1 << v)], v))
    return len(st), occ, np.array(dele), np.array(ins)


def log_trel(ch, logLam):
    """log(t_rel · c)，logLam[v] = log Λ_v。"""
    N, occ, dele, ins = ch
    lw = occ @ logLam; lw -= lw.max(); pi = np.exp(lw); pi /= pi.sum()
    Q = np.zeros((N, N))
    Q[dele[:, 0], dele[:, 1]] = 1.0
    Q[ins[:, 0], ins[:, 1]] = np.exp(logLam[ins[:, 2]])
    np.fill_diagonal(Q, -Q.sum(1))
    d = np.sqrt(pi); S = d[:, None] * Q / d[None, :]; S = (S + S.T) / 2
    ev = np.linalg.eigvalsh(-S)
    return -np.log(np.sort(ev)[1])


def alpha(ch, e, L1=np.log(1e6), L2=np.log(1e9)):
    e = np.asarray(e, float)
    return (log_trel(ch, e * L2) - log_trel(ch, e * L1)) / (L2 - L1)


def search(H, restarts=6, grid=8, rng=random.Random(0)):
    ch = chain(H); k = H.number_of_nodes(); best = (-1, None)
    for _ in range(restarts):
        e = [rng.randint(0, grid) for _ in range(k)]
        cur = alpha(ch, np.array(e) / grid); improved = True
        while improved:
            improved = False
            for v in range(k):
                for val in range(grid + 1):
                    if val == e[v]: continue
                    e2 = e.copy(); e2[v] = val; a = alpha(ch, np.array(e2) / grid)
                    if a > cur + 1e-9: e, cur, improved = e2, a, True
        if cur > best[0]: best = (cur, list(np.array(e) / grid))
    return best


if __name__ == "__main__":
    P4 = nx.path_graph(4)
    print(f"校验：分裂竞争（P4，e=(1/2,1,1,1/2)）α = {alpha(chain(P4), [0.5, 1, 1, 0.5]):.4f}（预期 0.5）\n")
    print("所有 k 点树上的最大减速指数 α(k)：")
    for k in range(2, 10):
        best = (-1, None, None)
        for T in nx.nonisomorphic_trees(k):
            T = nx.convert_node_labels_to_integers(T)
            a, e = search(T, restarts=3 if k >= 8 else 6)
            if a > best[0]: best = (a, e, sorted(d for _, d in T.degree()))
        print(f"  k={k}: α = {best[0]:.3f}   度序列 {best[2]}   指数 {best[1]}", flush=True)
    print("\n随机弦图（含非树）上的最大 α：")
    from chordal_decomposition_check import random_connected_chordal
    rng = random.Random(1)
    for k in [6, 8, 10]:
        best = -1
        for _ in range(12):
            G = nx.convert_node_labels_to_integers(random_connected_chordal(k, rng))
            best = max(best, search(G, restarts=2)[0])
        print(f"  k={k}: α = {best:.3f}", flush=True)
